**Date:** 2026-06-10
**Decision:** Two Base UI Select conventions locked after live user testing, extending the 2026-06-02 `items` decision: (1) **a controlled Select's empty value is `null`, never `undefined`** — `value={x || undefined}` mounts the component *uncontrolled*, and the first pick flips it to controlled (React/Base UI console warning). Five sites fixed (`377b412`): med + condition inline-field selects, condition log-change status/severity/doctor. (2) **Every Select whose values differ from labels passes `items`** is now satisfied repo-wide — the med log-change dialog (field/status/doctor/visit), med form (form/category), and med filter were the last holdouts (`656f3b4`); the doctor select was rendering a raw uuid on its trigger.
**Also:** the §6.5:1410 single-letter type prefixes (`D` doctor, `V` visit) were reading as typos as bare muted letters — now a small circled badge via shared `components/entity-type-glyph.tsx` (aria-hidden; `letter` typed `"D" | "V"`). `§` keeps its plain treatment (symbol, matches citation pills). One further missed "Dr Dr." fixed in med linked-context.
**Reasoning:** all three are conventions the remaining ~9 Phase D entities clone; locking them now (with the gotchas encoded in `.claude/skills/verify-ui/`) prevents per-entity rediscovery. Verified by the 20-step Playwright walkthrough (console silent; trigger labels asserted).

**Date:** 2026-06-10
**Decision:** Phase D — **Allergy vertical complete** (API · form · list · detail · `allergy_changes` log · live `§ allergy:` pill), fourth state vertical, cloned off the Condition tree.
**Context:** The spec is thinner here than for any prior entity: **Allergies is not a wiki rail item** — §6.4:1278 powers exactly four state lists (Medications/Conditions/Doctors/Family history) and §3's nine-item rail confirms it; the Allergies list is reached from the patient profile's `Allergies · N list →` link (§6.10:1678,1685). So §6.4 defines no Allergy grouping/card/filter, §6.5:1402 gives one emphasis line, and §6.12:1848 a one-line field draft. The main calls were the list-page treatment, the change-logged field set, and what to do about a Linked-context spec line with no stored data behind it.
**Choice:** (1) **List page built anyway** at `/patient/[id]/allergies` on the §6.4 template — it's the §6.10 link target, the `+ Add` host, and the detail-page parent; without it the vertical has no browse surface until the profile ships. (2) **Grouping by status: ACTIVE + SUSPECTED expanded, RESOLVED + DISPROVED collapsed** — suspected sits next to active because §4:271 calls it medically meaningful ("the AI should treat it differently"); same ordering drives STATUS_OPTIONS, the subtitle, and the serializer's existing sort. Filter = single `All categories ▾` (drug/food/environmental/other), mirroring Conditions. (3) **Card = Condition's three-line shape** with allergy emphasis: substance + category pill / severity · reaction + status pill / first-noted + confirmed-by; `unknown` severity is suppressed as noise (it's the default-when-absent per §4:261) — same suppression in the pill popover. (4) **Change-logged fields = status + severity only** (§6.5:1402 History axes); PATCH owns substance/category/reaction/firstNoted/confirmedBy/notes and refuses the two logged fields with per-field guidance. `allergy_changes.field` is a **plain text column** (no pgEnum, unlike condition_changes) — the Zod discriminated union is the sole guard; no migration (schema locked), change-entry label map falls back to uppercasing unknown values. (5) **Category required on the form** with no default — deviation from §6.12:1848, which stars only Substance, but §4:259 makes the column NOT NULL with no DB default and the field "scopes AI risk reasoning"; defaulting to `other` would pollute the axis. Status/Severity DO have DB defaults (active/unknown) so they default in place — no NOT_SET sentinel anywhere in this vertical. (6) **Confirmed-by omitted from the Add form** — joins the standing doctor-picker carryover (decisions 2026-06-09 alt-d); settable post-create via the `select-doctor` inline variant, scope-checked server-side at create AND update. (7) **Linked context section omitted entirely** — §6.5:1402 says "Linked: medications flagged for interaction," but §4:270 deliberately keeps cross-reactions as AI reasoning-time work (no stored med↔allergy edge exists to query). Until the insight generator (Phase E) produces these, rendering the section would mean an empty dashed box; §6.5:1386's omission rule (LifestyleProfile precedent) applies. Revisit when insights land. (8) **Prominent cards = category + severity, not substance** — §6.5:1402 lists substance among the prominent fields, but substance IS the H1 page title; repeating it in a card is noise. Recorded deviation. Category's prominent card is inline-editable in edit mode (first PATCH-editable prominent field — the InlineField renders inside the card; no structural change needed).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) No list page until the §6.10 profile ships — rejected; the vertical would be unreachable and untestable, and `+ Add` needs a home. (b) Add Allergies to the rail — rejected; §3's nine-item rail is locked and the rail itself isn't built yet (no rail exists to deviate from — the page is URL/profile-reached either way). (c) Category optional with `other` default — rejected (pollutes the AI-reasoning axis). (d) Render Linked context with AI-derived interactions now — rejected; that's the Phase E insight generator, and faking it with substring matching against med names invents clinical claims (hard-rule territory). (e) pgEnum migration for `allergy_changes.field` — rejected; schema locked since Phase A, Zod covers it, sign-off not worth a migration for a 2-value set.
**Reasoning:** Fourth proof of the state template — the clone needed zero structural change, only per-entity field sets, confirming the Phase D economics. The thin-spec calls all resolve toward either a locked schema (§4 wins over a §6.12 "rough draft"), an established precedent (Condition's grouping/card/dialog shapes), or the spec's own omission rule (§6.5:1386). **Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean; 16-case curl E2E on arogya-dev (create minimal/full, missing-category 400, PATCH allow/refuse/category-null-refuse, status change + 409 no-op + severity change + bad-field 400, by-slug round-trip, foreign-doctor 400 w/ per-field message, in-scope doctor PATCH, status filter, out-of-scope delete 404, delete 204 → 404); 26-step Playwright walkthrough green (grouping/collapse, filter URL round-trip + bogus-param fallback, form errors/dirty-cancel/save-and-add-another reset, edit-mode lock + hint, blur-commit, doctor-label select + D-glyph link + no "Dr Dr.", log-change strikethrough + header pill update + current-status exclusion, by-slug payload, 404 probe, delete copy/redirect). Console silent (one expected 404 from the bogus-id probe). All ZZZ rows cleaned.

**Date:** 2026-06-10
**Decision:** Phase D — **Lifestyle + FamilyHistory verticals complete** (the last two state entities), closing the §6.4/§6.5/§6.12 state-template column. Both are template *variations* rather than clones: FamilyHistory is the no-change-log state entity; Lifestyle is the lazily-created singleton with no Add form.
**Context:** FamilyHistory is fully spec'd (§4:541-564, §6.4:1322/1336/1343, §6.5:1404, §6.12:1850) minus a detail-page title and a grouping bucket for `child`. Lifestyle's spec has a real hole: §6.12 defines **no "Add lifestyle" form** (the profile is onboarding-populated in Phase E), §4:534 doesn't say *which* fields are change-logged, and §6.5:1403's locked 4-column strip omits `exerciseIntensity`. Both verticals cloned off the Allergy tree; the calls below fill the gaps.
**Choice — Lifestyle:**
(1) **PATCH /api/lifestyle is an upsert** (no create route, no `[id]`/`by-slug` segments — the citation slug is the fixed `lifestyle:profile`, so the pill resolves through the plain GET, which 404s while no row exists). The page renders fine with a null profile; the first write creates the row. (2) **Change-logged ("trend") fields = the seven §4:538 axes**: dietPattern / exercisePattern / sleepPattern / exerciseIntensity / stressLevel / tobaccoUse / alcoholUse. dietRestrictions (tag list), stressContext (the §6.5:1415 companion quote), and notes are plain PATCH-editable. (3) **First-population rule** — the singleton's create-form analog: a trend field passes through PATCH **while its current value is null** (and idempotent re-sends are tolerated); once populated it 400s with per-field guidance toward POST /api/lifestyle/changes, and the UI mirrors this (empty trend fields render inline editors in edit mode; populated ones lock with the hint). First population writes **no change-log row** — there's no change yet; the log starts at the first real transition. (4) **No-op changes 409 for all seven fields**, not just a status — a `moderate → moderate` row would pollute the exact trend story the log exists to tell. The dialog's enum selects exclude the current value; the three pattern fields get a **textarea prefilled with the current narrative** (trend changes are usually edits of the prior text), so the only 409 path is submitting unchanged (surfaced as the stale-value branch). (5) **lifestyle_changes is keyed by patient_id only** (no profile FK per §4:534) — change queries scope directly, and change-create lazily inserts the profile row inside its transaction. (6) **exerciseIntensity renders as a sub-line inside the Exercise narrative block** — §6.5:1403's strip is locked at 4 columns (stress/tobacco/alcohol/restrictions) and doesn't place intensity. (7) **No `…` actions menu** — the menu exists for state transitions + Delete (§6.5:1384); a singleton profile has neither (no delete story/API; test rows this session were cleared via SQL). (8) **dietRestrictions edits as comma-separated text** → trimmed `string[]` on the wire (v1 simplification; a real tags input is polish).
**Choice — FamilyHistory:**
(9) **Detail H1 = conditionName** with the relation-type pill in the status-pill slot (FH has no status) and a "Father · onset age 65" subtitle; prominent Current cards = who (relationSpecific, falling back to the relation label) + age of onset; grid = relation select + outcome. §6.5:1404 lists fields without naming a title — condition-first is the clinically scannable identity and mirrors Allergy's substance-as-H1 precedent. (10) **Everything is inline-editable** — no change log (§4:558) means no locked fields, no log-change provider, no History section (omitted entirely), and a PATCH with no refusal map; the new `number` InlineField variant carries ageOfOnset as a wire number. `ageOfOnset` bounded 0-130 with a "That looks like a year, not an age" teaching error. (11) **Grouping: PARENTS / SIBLINGS / CHILDREN expanded; GRANDPARENTS / OTHER (aunt_uncle + cousin + other) collapsed** — §6.4:1336 doesn't mention `child` (its sample data has none); children are first-degree relatives, so they get their own expanded bucket rather than being buried under OTHER. No filter pills (§6.4:1343). (12) **Linked context = render-time name match** against the patient's own conditions (case-insensitive substring, either direction), section omitted when no matches — implements §6.5:1404's "could surface the patient's own conditions that match" without storing an edge (backlinks-derived tripwire). This is a *name* match presented as "Also in this record: X", not a clinical claim. (13) **bySlug reuses `familyHistorySlug()`** from the serializer (the slug is compound — `slugify(relation-conditionName)` — so re-deriving the construction would invite drift).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) An explicit "Set up lifestyle profile" form/route — rejected; invents a §6.12 surface the spec deliberately lacks, and inline first-population covers manual entry until onboarding lands. (b) Change-log *all* lifestyle fields — rejected; routes a stress-context typo fix through a clinical-event dialog. (c) Change-log only the four enums — rejected; the §4:538 demo case ("patient cut sugar in March 2026") IS a dietPattern change, so the narrative patterns must be loggable. (d) Log first population as `— → value` change rows — rejected; seven "set to" entries of onboarding noise before any real change. (e) FH `child` under OTHER — rejected (buries first-degree relatives). (f) FH H1 = relationSpecific ("Father") — rejected; two entries for the same relative would render identical pages, and the condition is what you scan for. (g) Exact-match-only FH linked context — substring chosen so "Heart attack" ↔ "Heart Attack (MI)" connect; tradeoff: short names can over-match, acceptable for a render-time hint.
**Reasoning:** These two close the state column with the template's two hardest variations, proving §6.5 absorbs "no History at all" and "narrative blocks + strip + lock-by-population" without structural change. The first-population rule keeps the trend story clean for the north-star demo while keeping manual entry possible pre-onboarding. **Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean; 30-case curl E2E on arogya-dev (FH 16: create full/minimal, year-as-age 400 w/ teaching message, strict-key 400, PATCH set/clear/null-relation-refused/empty-400, by-slug round-trip + miss, bad-uuid 400, delete 204→404; Lifestyle 14: GET 404-when-absent, first-population upsert, locked-trend 400 w/ rejectedFields, idempotent re-send, companions set/clear, bad enums 400, change w/ server oldValue + noon-UTC changedAt, no-op 409, set-to-null-oldValue change, non-trend field rejected by union, post-change lock); 30-step Playwright walkthrough green (FH grouping/collapse incl. GRANDPARENTS default-collapsed, form errors/add-another-reset/dirty-cancel, inline number-edit + 999 server error surfaced inline, relation select shows labels, delete copy/redirect, 404 probe; Lifestyle narrative blocks + strip + stress quote, history strikethrough/reason/set-to, diet locked in edit + exercise first-population + stressContext companion edit, dialog enum exclusion + prefill + stale-value branch on no-op, hint opens dialog; both empty states probed after cleanup). Console silent except expected probe 400/404/409s. All test rows cleaned (FH via API, lifestyle via SQL — no delete API). The one step-1 walkthrough ❌ was a test-script selector bug (`h1 ~ p`); re-probed green. **Uncommitted at handoff — pending user review.**
**/check addendum (same session):** three review fixes applied before commit — (i) FH linked-context name match now requires the shorter string ≥3 chars (a 1-2 char condition name as the contained side matched nearly everything → junk "Also in this record" links); (ii) FH form's client-side ageOfOnset refine now mirrors the server's ≤130 bound (999 previously passed the client regex and bounced off the server instead of erroring inline); (iii) the lifestyle inline-field lock error shown on a stale page now says "use + Log a change in History" instead of surfacing the API's "POST /api/lifestyle/changes" guidance (7.1 voice — endpoint paths aren't user copy). Also surfaced: stale `ZZZ`/`ZZZ-smoke` conditions from *earlier* sessions still in arogya-dev (prior handoffs recorded them cleaned) — left untouched pending user confirmation.

**Date:** 2026-06-10
**Decision:** Phase D — **Visit vertical complete** (API · Log-visit form · §6.6 timeline · §6.7 event detail · Outcomes from backlinks · live `§ visit:` pill), the **first event entity** — proves the new 6.6/6.7 template pair before Lab/Symptom/Report/Journal clone it.
**Context:** Events differ structurally from the five state verticals: no `*_changes` table (a visit IS the event), Outcomes replaces History, time replaces status as the organizing axis, and visits don't *contain* outcomes — they're back-referenced via `linked_visit_id` on medication_changes / lab_reports / reports / symptom_episodes (Phase 4 Visit note). Spec gaps hit: §6.12:1855 + §6.7:1505 both mention a visit **duration**, but the locked Phase 4 table has no such column; §6.6 wants a **warm/terra tint** the periwinkle-on-stone palette doesn't have; §6.6 wants **clickable result badges** inside cards that are already whole-card anchors.
**Choice:**
(1) **Full PATCH surface, no refusal map** — every column is plainly editable (doctorId scope-checked like create); §6.7's "Edit toggles in-place editing" with no change log means corrections edit the row. The inline-field clone collapses the per-entity select variants into one generic `select` variant taking `options` as a prop (visit edits select across doctor/type/status sets).
(2) **Form renders the full Phase 4 field set (9 fields), not §6.12's 5-field rough draft** — chief complaint / diagnosis / next steps / status / notes exist on the locked schema and the detail page would otherwise be their only entry point. **Duration omitted everywhere — SPEC GAP**: §6.12:1855 and §6.7's capture-context line both name it; Phase 4 has no column. Needs either a sign-off'd schema addition or a doc-fix (recommended: doc-fix; duration is low-signal for synthesis).
(3) **Doctor is a required plain Select** (name + specialty) — first form where the doctor ref can't be deferred (visits.doctor_id NOT NULL); the §6.12 rich-autocomplete carryover stands. **Zero-doctors guard**: /visits/new renders an "add the doctor first" CTA instead of a form with an unfillable required field.
(4) **Timeline**: month sections non-collapsible (§6.6 marks only symptom-type groups collapsible); "Show N earlier visits ▾" splits at **whole-month boundaries** once ≥10 visits are visible (server-renders all, client island reveals); subtitle = `N in last 90 days · M doctors · most recent X ago [· next <date>]` — the `next` segment surfaces scheduled future visits (§4:357's prep journey); **non-completed visits get a status pill** on card + detail (§6.6's card sketch assumes completed; scheduled/cancelled need signaling). formatRelative is never fed a future date (its contract flattens them to "today").
(5) **Most-recent tint + significant-outcome tint ride `--accent` (periwinkle)** — §6.6/§6.7 call for warm/terra matching the dashboard's Top Insight card, but no terra token exists and the dashboard doesn't either. Tokenized, so it's a one-place swap — **needs a user call** whether to add a terra/highlight token (CLAUDE.md color-decision rule).
(6) **Result badges are styled spans inside the whole-card anchor, NOT links** — §6.6:1488 says badges are clickable, but nested anchors are invalid HTML (same constraint that keeps list-card entity refs inert, 2026-06-09 alt-d); the detail page's Outcomes rows carry the link-outs (`View medication →`). Recorded deviation.
(7) **Outcomes section** renders med changes (`↑ name old → new`, italicized reason), lab reports (`≡ <type> ordered`), reports (`+ title`); **first dose/status med change gets the one-per-page significant tint**; lab/report rows stay link-less until those verticals land. **Section omitted when empty** (§6.5:1386 rationale) — outcome links are created from the entity side (the med log-change dialog's visit picker, live since Phase C), not from the visit page.
(8) **Linked context = FK-linked symptom episodes only**, with §6.7 relative-to-page framing ("3 days after visit"); **pure-temporal neighbors (no FK) are deliberately not queried** — date-window inference is the synthesis agent's job (Phase 5), not a page heuristic. Empty until the Symptom vertical ships a create path; section omitted when empty.
(9) **Slug = the serializer's ISO date** (`visit:2026-04-30`, locked in Phase B) — §6.7:1551 suggests `[date]-[summary]`, but matching the serializer is what makes pills resolve (construction-round-trip convention); same `-2` collision-suffix limitation as the other entities. **Breadcrumb shows the date slug**, not the uuid prefix (closer to §6.7's shell than the state pages' uuid-slice; harmless inconsistency to sweep later).
(10) **Markdown bodies render**: summary/nextSteps go through react-markdown (already a dep) with constrained typography so §6.7's "bolded key data within prose" (`**152/95**`) actually bolds — first non-chat markdown surface; notes stays plain pre-wrap like every Notes section.
(11) **Unlocks landed with the vertical**: doctor list/detail last-visit derivation + the 409 delete_blocked path are now reachable (verified live); doctor-detail and med-detail linked-visit rows got their queued `Link` wrappers; `§ visit:` pill resolves via `/api/visits/by-slug/[slug]` (doctor joined for the §6.7 title shape). Med change-entry's "Linked: V Visit ·…" annotation stays inert text (the med detail's Linked-context section carries the link) — minor carryover.
(12) **Delete** detaches outcomes (every backlink is set-null); the dialog copy states the count ("N linked outcomes will stay in the record but lose their link").
**Alternatives considered:** (a) §6.12's literal 5-field form — rejected; the schema's clinical fields would have no entry path at log time. (b) Add a `duration` column — rejected without sign-off (schema locked since Phase A). (c) Introduce a terra token unilaterally — rejected; color values are an ask-first item. (d) Badges as nested links via z-index/stopPropagation — rejected; invalid-HTML hack for a navigation the detail page already provides. (e) Outcomes empty-state CTA on the visit page — rejected; the write path lives on the entity side and a dead-end dashed box invites confusion. (f) Date-window "symptoms following this visit" query — rejected; invents temporal correlation in the UI layer that the synthesis agent should reason about (and cite) instead.
**Reasoning:** The event template's load-bearing differences (Outcomes-not-History, time-not-status, backlink-derived content) all survived contact with a real entity without structural compromise — Lab/Symptom/Report/Journal can now clone this tree the way Condition→Allergy cloned Medication's. The Outcomes pipeline completes a demo-moment prerequisite: a dose change logged at a visit now renders on the visit card (`↑ Amlodipine 5 mg → 10 mg`), the visit page (with the doctor's quoted reason), and the med's History — the cross-doctor synthesis story has a navigable substrate.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean; 24-check curl smoke (create minimal/full/scheduled, foreign-doctor 400 w/ doctorId field error, strict-key 400 incl. a probed `duration` key, sourceReportId rejected, doctor filter + bad-filter 400, PATCH narrative/type-null/doctorId-null-refused/foreign-doctor/empty-body, by-slug round-trip + miss 404, delete 204→404, med change accepted `linkedVisitId`); 28-step Playwright walkthrough effectively green — 25 ✅ + 3 ❌ that were all test-script bugs (two wrong selectors re-probed ✅; one expected-count that forgot a stale smoke row — the app's `· 3 visits` header was correct). Walkthrough covered: month grouping, date column + dotted separator, most-recent tint (and non-tint on others), ↑ badge, SCHEDULED pill, show-earlier reveal at the month boundary, doctor filter URL round-trip + bogus-param fallback, §6.7 title/pill/subtitle, date-slug breadcrumb, markdown `<strong>`, Outcomes tint/reason/link-out → med detail → linked-context back to the visit, inline edit blur-commit, select labels (no raw enums/uuids), required-date refusal, doctor last-visit + 409 delete_blocked, by-slug payload, form error/aria-invalid/add-another-reset/save-redirect/dirty-cancel, delete dialog outcome-detach copy → empty state. Console silent. All ZZZ rows cleaned via API; the earlier curl-smoke artifacts (2 visits on a real doctor + one no-op dose change-row on a real medication) removed — visits table left empty, medication history restored. **Uncommitted — pending user review.**

**Follow-up (2026-06-11, user review):** Two open calls resolved + one bug fixed.
- **Duration gap → doc-fix applied** (user: "fix up the doc"). design.md §6.12:1852 (Log-visit field set) and §6.7:1500 (capture context) edited to drop "Duration" and match the locked Phase 4 table; revisit as a v1.5 schema addition if visit length proves worth capturing. No code change.
- **Terra tint → keep `--accent`** (user: "color is fine as of now"). Most-recent card + significant-outcome tints stay on the periwinkle `--accent` token; no terra token added. Tokenized, so still a one-place swap if a warm token lands later.
- **Bug fix — "last visit 20h ago" for a visit logged today.** `visit_date` is date-only; the doctor card, doctor-detail Current section, and visits-timeline subtitle all anchored it to local midnight and fed it to `formatRelative`, whose sub-day branch ("Nh ago", "Nm ago") then reported the hours since midnight — so a visit logged this evening read "20h ago". Root cause: a calendar date has no time-of-day, so hour-level precision is meaningless on it. Fix: new `formatRelativeDate(d, now?)` in lib/datetime.ts — day-granularity relative formatting ("today" / "yesterday" / "N days ago", symmetric "tomorrow" / "in N days" for scheduled future visits), never sub-day. Applied to the three date-only sites. Change-log entries keep `formatRelative` (those are real `timestamptz` columns; the only synthetic-time case is a backdated-to-today change via the noon-UTC picker, pre-existing and out of scope). `tsc` clean; logic table-tested (today→"today", +4d→"in 4 days", 2mo→"2 months ago").

**`/check` review + fixes (2026-06-11):** spec-compliance + principal review run on the Visit vertical; no blockers, four engineering fixes applied + re-verified (`tsc`/`eslint` clean):
1. **90-day subtitle window** (visits timeline) computed in browser-local basis, not UTC — `today` came from `todayLocal()` but the cutoff used `toISOString()`, so an evening-US session could miscount a visit at the ~90-day edge. Now both bounds share `todayLocal()`'s basis via local `Date(y, m, d-90)` arithmetic.
2. **`nextScheduled`** found via `reduce`-min over future scheduled visits instead of `.at(-1)` on the desc-sorted list — the old form silently depended on the query's ORDER BY; the invariant now lives at the use-site.
3. **Most-recent tint** derived from the FILTERED view (`filtered.find(v => v.visitDate <= today)`), not the full set — filtering to one doctor now tints *their* latest visit; previously the tint vanished whenever the global-latest visit was filtered out.
4. **Outcome glyph is field-aware** — `↑` for dose/frequency changes, **`↔` for med status/prescriber transitions** (was `↑` for everything; `↑ Statin discontinued` implied the wrong direction). Dose magnitude direction is deliberately NOT inferred (free-text doses don't parse), so `↑` stays a generic change arrow for dose/frequency. `OutcomeItem.glyph` union widened to `"↑" | "↔" | "≡" | "+"`.
**Cleanup-safety lesson (process):** the post-verify cleanup blanket-deleted ALL visits instead of only test-created IDs, which removed a real Dr. Sharma test visit (recreated afterward at user request). Event entities can't carry a `ZZZ` name-prefix the way state rows do (no name column), so the verify-ui "delete by ZZZ prefix" convention doesn't cover them — **track created event IDs explicitly and delete only those; never blanket-delete an event table when user rows may exist.** Gotcha logged for the verify-ui skill.

**Date:** 2026-06-15
**Decision:** Phase D — **Lab vertical complete** (LabReport + LabResult: API · Log-lab-report form with markers sub-form · §6.6 month-grouped timeline · §6.7 event detail with read-only MARKERS table + `+ Log a correction` · live `§ lab-report:` pill), the **second event entity** and the **first one→many split** — clones the Visit 6.6/6.7 tree and adds the read-only-table + correction mechanic the remaining events (Symptom/Report) reuse.
**Context:** A lab is one document (LabReport) owning many measured values (LabResult) — the split that makes "creatinine over 12 months" a clean time-series query (§4:369). Structural firsts vs. Visit: a nested create (report + N markers in one transaction), a body that is a **read-only table** (§6.7: "LabReport markers are read-only — corrections via `+ Log a correction`") rather than an inline-editable narrative, and a result row that has **no `*_changes` table** despite being correctable. Spec wrinkles hit: §6.7 wants an **amber △ warning glyph** the periwinkle+neutrals+destructive palette has no token for; §6.7's "SLIGHTLY HIGH / LOW / CRITICAL" pill register implies a slightly-vs-severely gradation the 4-value flag enum (normal/low/high/critical) doesn't carry; §6.7 lists both a `Lipid panel` **title** and a `lipid panel` **type pill** that would duplicate for labs.
**Choice:**
(1) **Routes split by resource name**: pages at `/patient/[id]/labs/*` (the rail label), API at `/api/lab-reports/*` (matches the `lab_reports`/`lab_results` tables), with marker corrections nested at `/api/lab-reports/[id]/results/[resultId]` (PATCH only). The `§ lab-report:` citation href points at `/labs/[id]`.
(2) **Correction = in-place overwrite of the LabResult row** — **FLAGGED DECISION.** There is no `lab_results_changes` table (Phase 4 gives results no change log), so `+ Log a correction` PATCHes the measured fields (value/unit/range/flag) in place; the marker *name* is immutable (it's the row identity). The dashed-border affordance is the friction signal ("rare amendment, not primary edit"), not append-only history. *Open question for the user:* is overwrite acceptable, or should a correction append a superseding row / carry an audit trail? Chose overwrite for v1 (no schema change; matches "markers are read-only, corrections are rare").
(3) **Edit boundary**: the detail Edit toggle touches **report-level fields only** (reportType, reportDate, labName, orderedBy, summary, notes) via the inline-field clone; the MARKERS table never enters inline-edit. So the report PATCH and the marker-correction PATCH are cleanly separate surfaces.
(4) **Form = §6.12 lab field set** (reportType · date received · labName · ordering doctor · markers sub-form · notes), source-file upload **deferred to the Phase E extraction path** (the preferred route for real PDFs). `useFieldArray` drives the marker rows; a single "Value" input per row is **split client-side into numeric `value` vs qualitative `valueText`** (§4:406) in the submit handler. `linkedVisitId` / `summary` / per-result `linkedCondition` are **not manual-form fields** — they're extraction/AI-set (linkedVisit also set from the ordering visit); summary stays editable post-hoc on the detail. Ordering doctor is **optional** (a patient may self-order), so unlike the Visit form there's no zero-doctors guard.
(5) **Flag pills** carry the **`destructive` token at two emphasis levels** — filled for `critical`, outline for `high`/`low` — with the `△` glyph; **no amber/warning token added** (a Phase-7 color decision, deferred per the CLAUDE.md ask-first rule). Pill labels: `high`→"Slightly high", `low`→"Slightly low", `critical`→"Critical" — the non-critical out-of-range flags take the softer "Slightly" register since the enum can't distinguish slightly-vs-severely; `normal`/null render no pill. Flagged table rows get a `bg-destructive/5` tint.
(6) **No most-recent tint** on lab cards — §6.6 applies the warm tint to Visits and Symptoms only (Journal and Labs are excluded). The flag pills carry the clinical signal instead.
(7) **Type pill folded into the title** — the §6.7 title IS the report type (`Lipid panel`, falling back to lab name / "Lab report"); a separate `lipid panel` pill beside it would just duplicate. Recorded deviation. The report **date is the row identity** (breadcrumb date-slug + "received {date}" subtitle), mirroring the Visit title's date.
(8) **Outcomes = flagged-marker summary** (`⚠ N markers flagged`) + a `View flagged markers →` anchor that **scrolls** to the MARKERS table (§6.7: scroll don't filter — normal markers stay visible beside flagged). Insight-generated outcomes are **Phase E** (omitted in v1); the brief flagged-row flash-on-arrival is **deferred polish** (rows already carry a tint). Section omitted when nothing flagged.
(9) **Linked context = monitored conditions + previous panels** — conditions referenced by the report's markers (`lab_results.linked_condition` backlinks, live into the state vault) and prior reports of the same `report_type` with relative-to-page framing ("2 months earlier"). Forward-links (later same-type panels) deliberately **not** surfaced — longitudinal trend reading is the synthesis agent's job (Phase 5). Section omitted when both strands empty.
(10) **Reuse over clone**: the generic `TimelineShowEarlier` (components/visits) is imported as-is (it's entity-agnostic, takes a `noun` prop) rather than re-cloned. **Delete cascades the marker rows** (FK onDelete:cascade); the dialog copy names the marker count since it's a harder-to-reverse loss than a visit's set-null detach.
(11) **`§ lab-report:` pill resolves** via `/api/lab-reports/by-slug/[slug]` (slug = ISO report date, serializer round-trip), with marker count + flagged count + ordering doctor joined for the popover; added as the 8th interactive entity config. The Visit Outcomes' lab badge (`≡ <type> ordered`) now **links live** to the lab detail (was inert pending this vertical).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) Append-only correction history for results — rejected for v1 (needs a `lab_results_changes` schema addition, locked since Phase A; flagged for user sign-off instead). (b) Inline-editable markers — rejected; directly contradicts §6.7's read-only locked decision. (c) A separate `lipid panel` type pill — rejected as pure duplication of the title. (d) An amber/warning token for the △ — rejected unilaterally (ask-first color rule); destructive-at-two-emphasis is the tokenized interim. (e) A visit-picker + summary field on the create form — rejected; §6.12's lab field set omits them and they're extraction-set, so the form stays faithful and lean. (f) Forward-linked later panels in Linked context — rejected; trend inference belongs to synthesis, not a page query.
**Reasoning:** The one→many event shape survived the 6.6/6.7 template without structural compromise — Symptom (type→episodes) can now clone this tree the way Allergy cloned Condition, and the read-only-table + correction-dialog mechanic is reusable for any event whose body is structured data rather than narrative. The flagged-marker pipeline feeds the demo moment: an out-of-range marker now renders on the timeline card, the detail Outcomes line, and (Phase E) will seed an insight — all citation-addressable via `§ lab-report:`.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` clean. 15-step Playwright walkthrough green (0 fail, 3 probes): timeline load + breadcrumb + Ask AI; form (no Ask AI, markers sub-form, flag select, save→redirect); timeline card marker-preview + `△` glyph; report-type filter URL round-trip + bogus-param fallback; §6.7 detail title/subtitle/read-only MARKERS table; SLIGHTLY HIGH pill + `⚠ 1 marker flagged` Outcomes + View-flagged link; `+ Log a correction` dialog amends a marker (high→critical, CRITICAL pill appears); report-level inline edit blur-commit; previous-panel Linked context; `by-slug` payload (+ 404 on miss); bogus-uuid detail 404. Console silent (lone 404 = the intentional bogus-id probe). All ZZZ rows + the diagnostic curl row deleted via API (cascade verified — labs table left as found). **Uncommitted — pending user review.**

**Follow-up (2026-06-16, user review + `/check`):** `/check` ran (no blockers); three minor DRY/defensive fixes applied + the two open product calls resolved.
- **`/check` fixes:** (1) numeric-value regex centralized — `NUMERIC_VALUE_RE` exported from `lib/schemas/forms/lab-report.ts`, imported by the correction dialog (was in 3 places); (2) `FLAG_SELECT_ITEMS` exported from `lab-options.ts`, shared by the form + correction dialog; (3) `createLabResultSchema` gained a `.refine` rejecting a body with **both** `value` and `valueText` (§4:406 split; verified 400). Plus: the Outcomes `⚠` forced to text presentation (`U+FE0E`) so it doesn't render as a colored emoji.
- **Lab correction → keep in-place overwrite** (user choice). No `lab_results_changes` table; a correction is a typo-fix, not a clinical event. Matches the locked Phase 4 schema; no change. (If amended values ever need traceability, that's a v1.5 schema addition.)
- **Warning color → `--warning` (amber) token ADDED** (user choice, supersedes the destructive-token interim). New `--warning` + `--warning-foreground` in app/globals.css (light `oklch(0.52 0.12 68)` warm ochre-amber, hue 68, desaturated to sit with the warm-stone neutrals per 7.2; dark `oklch(0.78 0.12 75)`, light-first caveat like the periwinkle dark ramp) wired through `@theme` as `--color-warning`. Usage: **critical stays on `destructive` (red, alarm); non-critical out-of-range flags (high/low) move to `--warning` (amber)** — the §6.7 "amber △ / worth-noting" register. Marker pills, the MARKERS table row-tint (severity-split: critical→`destructive/5`, mild→`warning/10`), and the Outcomes summary (red iff any critical, else amber via a new `criticalCount` prop) all reflect this. **Also pre-unblocks the §6.8 insight "attention" severity dots** (amber), which would have needed the same token. Verified live: slightly-high pill renders `rgb(149,89,0)` (warm amber), critical `rgb(197,55,50)` (red) — distinct; `tsc`/`eslint` clean; 15-step walkthrough still green.

**Date:** 2026-06-16
**Decision:** Phase D — **Symptom vertical complete** (SymptomType + SymptomEpisode: API · Log-symptom form with inline `+ Create new` type · §6.6 **type-grouped** timeline · §6.7 episode detail with "Captured at this episode" linked vitals · **new SymptomType state-detail page** · live `§ symptom:` + `§ symptom-episode:` pills), plus the **VitalReading create-only dependency** (API + Log-reading form, no list/detail). The **third event entity** and the **inverted one→many split** — clones the Lab one→many tree but flips which side is the event.
**Context:** Symptoms invert the Lab shape: the **child** (SymptomEpisode) is the timestamped event with the timeline card + detail page, while the **parent** (SymptomType) is the stable identity that groups the timeline and carries `status`/`linked_condition`/`notes`. Three structural firsts: a **timeline grouped by type, not by month** (§6.6 — the only rail item that does); an **inline-type-create inside the child POST** (`+ Create new` resolves the parent atomically in one transaction); and a **bidirectional cross-entity link** (`symptom_episodes.linked_vital_ids[]` ↔ `vital_readings.linked_symptom_id`). VitalReading rides along because the episode's `●` linked-vital pill needs real readings to point at, and §4:412 makes vitals a quick-log surface with no detail page.
**Choice:**
(1) **SymptomType gets a state-detail page** (`/symptoms/types/[typeId]`) — **the one addition beyond the 6.6/6.7 wireframes, user-approved 2026-06-16.** The wireframes specify only the type-grouped timeline + episode detail, but `SymptomType.status` (active/monitoring/resolved), `linked_condition`, `body_area`, `first_noted`, and `notes` have no other edit home — and the timeline's own "active types expanded, others collapsed" behavior depends on a `status` that, without this page, could only be set at create and never transitioned to resolved. Built on the §6.5 state template (header + status/body-area pills, inline-editable Current grid, markdown Notes, episode stream). It's the target of the episode detail's parent-link chip and the timeline's "View type →" link. **No change log** (no `symptom_type_changes` table — §4), so status is a plain PATCH, unlike Condition's change-logged status.
(2) **Routes split by resource name**: pages at `/patient/[id]/symptoms/*` (rail label), API at `/api/symptom-types/*` + `/api/symptom-episodes/*` (matches the two tables). Types have **no create route** — a type is born inside an episode POST (`createSymptomEpisodeSchema` takes exactly one of `symptomTypeId` | `newType`, resolved in a transaction); types expose only list (form autocomplete) + PATCH/DELETE + by-slug.
(3) **Linked-vital pill is non-navigating** — **FLAGGED.** §6.6 says the `●` pill is "clickable → navigate to the linked vital reading," but VitalReading has **no detail page** in v1 (checklist item 11 — vitals are create-only; §6.6's rail lists five event items, vitals not among them). So the pill (on cards) and the "Captured at this episode" rows (on detail) render the reading's value/context/flag inline as display-only spans — same call as the Visit result-badges-as-spans. The `§ vital:` citation pill likewise **stays inert** (no detail page to resolve to), consistent with the citation-pill inert-when-no-page rule.
(4) **No inline temporal-correlation tag** on episode cards (§6.6's "Day after dose change" terra label) — deliberately omitted, same reasoning as the Visit linked-context (decisions.md 2026-06-13 (f)): cross-entity date-window correlation is the **synthesis agent's** job to reason about and *cite* (Phase 5), not a page heuristic that would invent uncited correlations. The most-recent warm tint (§6.6, Visits+Symptoms) IS applied — the single globally-most-recent episode card.
(5) **Form = §6.12 set + the Visit-precedent extras**: Symptom type (Select + `+ Create new` revealing name/body-area) · started-at (datetime-local, default now) · duration · severity · description · **triggers + relief** (added beyond §6.12's draft, same call as the Visit form — clinical fields need a log-time entry path, decisions.md 2026-06-13 (a)) · linked vital · notes. **Linked vital is a single optional select** in v1 (the `uuid[]` column supports many; multi-link is set later by extraction/AI) — flagged simplification. Atomic episode+type create means no zero-types guard (unlike the Visit zero-doctors guard).
(6) **VitalReading = create-only** (`/api/vital-readings` POST/GET/DELETE, `/vitals/new` form): no timeline/detail page, no PATCH (readings are immutable — a mistake is deleted and re-entered, §4:433). GET-list exists solely for the symptom form's linked-vital autocomplete; DELETE for the immutable-correction path. **The form's entry point (rail/dashboard quick-action) is Phase E** — for now the page is URL-reachable and the symptom form links to it; flagged so it's not read as missing. The BP-style two-value reading reveals a secondary (diastolic) input; unit pre-fills per reading type.
(7) **Episode inline-edit adds `datetime` + `number` variants** over the visit/condition inline-field (timestamptz started/ended need ISO↔datetime-local conversion; duration is an integer). Full PATCH surface is inline-editable on the detail (`symptom_type_id` excluded — an episode belongs to its type for life; `linked_vital_ids` excluded — no array editor in v1, set at create).
(8) **Citation pills**: `§ symptom:<slugify(name)>` → `/symptoms/types/[id]` and `§ symptom-episode:<ISO-date>` → `/symptoms/[id]`, added as the 9th + 10th interactive configs (by-slug round-trip, same collision-suffix limit as visit/lab). The parser already accepted hyphenated entity types (`symptom-episode`), so no parser change.
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **No SymptomType page** (timeline + episode only, strict wireframes) — rejected; orphans status/linked-condition with no edit path and breaks the timeline's status-driven expansion. (b) **Inline-edit type fields on the timeline section header** — rejected; invents an interaction not in the spec and complicates the island. (c) **Two-request episode create** (POST type, then POST episode) — rejected for the inline-type case; the atomic single-transaction POST avoids a partial-failure orphan type. (d) **Multi-select linked vitals on the form** — deferred to v1.5/extraction; single-select covers the demo case (the BP reading taken during the dizziness episode). (e) **A standalone Vitals timeline/list page** — rejected as over-scope (checklist item 11 flags it explicitly); vitals surface only as `●` pills.
**Reasoning:** The inverted one→many survived the 6.6/6.7 template — the event-detail tree cloned cleanly from Visit/Lab, and the only genuinely new surface (the SymptomType state-detail) reused the §6.5 state template wholesale, so the two template families compose rather than conflict. The vertical feeds the north-star demo directly: a dizziness episode now links the low-BP reading taken at the same time (`● BP 88/55`), renders it warm-tinted in "Captured at this episode" when flagged, and is citation-addressable via `§ symptom-episode:` — the substrate for "his dizziness episodes cluster on mornings after he skipped the BP medication" is now navigable and synthesizable.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean (0 warnings — both new forms use `useWatch` not the React-Compiler-incompatible `watch`); `next build` green (all 9 new routes registered); `citation-parser:check` 11/11. **Browser walkthrough (verify-ui) NOT yet run** — there is no seed symptom/vital data, so a live walkthrough would drive the create flows; offered to the user. **Uncommitted — pending user review.**

**Follow-up (2026-06-17, user — VitalReading entry point):** Vital readings have **no wiki list page or in-app entry point** in Phase D, per §6.6 (vitals are designed as **chat-quick-log primary**, §5.3). The structured `/vitals/new` form ships as the manual fallback but is **URL-accessible only** during Phase D. The symptom form's "log a reading first…" hint **stays plain text** — making it a link would contradict the chat-first design intent (and would *teach* users a form-based path that the Phase E chat path is meant to supersede; learned paths stick). **Rejected** adding a "+ Log reading" affordance to the symptoms timeline: attaching vitals to symptoms *because vitals lack their own surface* is a cross-entity UI fix that compounds — the same logic would justify scattering cross-entity actions everywhere and erode each surface's focus. The symptoms timeline is for symptoms. The transient journey gap is a **sequencing artifact, not an architectural one** (same posture as the Ask AI button being inert during Phase C items 3–5) — accept the deferred gap rather than build bridge UI that needs teardown later.
**Phase E audit reminder:** when chat quick-log lands, explicitly verify end-to-end that **vital-reading-shaped inputs** ("BP 152/95 this morning") flow **router → extraction → confirmation → persist** correctly (path specified in §5.4 + §5.5) — i.e. confirm vitals reach their intended primary entry point.

**Phase E idea (2026-06-17, user — symptom↔condition duplicates):** The symptom/condition boundary is fuzzy for a class of complaints (pain, fatigue, swelling, headaches) — a user may log e.g. "Knee pain" as BOTH a symptom (the experienced complaint, tracked as episodes) and a condition (a diagnosis/state). The model is working as designed — a symptom is a *manifestation of* a condition, and the bridge already exists (`symptom_types.linked_condition` + the Condition page's "related symptoms" §3 page model). But **nothing prevents or reconciles the duplication**: there's no create-time nudge when a new symptom name matches an existing condition (or vice versa). **Decided NOT to fix in Phase D** — a name-matching UI hint is fuzzy-matching scope creep, and reconciliation is exactly the **insight/synthesis layer's** job (Phase E): an insight that reasons over the full vault should surface *"'Knee pain' is logged as both a symptom and a condition — consider linking or consolidating them,"* consistent with the product philosophy (structured layer captures, AI reconciles/flags). Audit/build candidate when the insight generator lands. (A create-time name-match hint is a separate v1.5 candidate if ever wanted.)

**Date:** 2026-06-17
**Decision:** **Symptom vertical — live-test review (8 UX fixes) + `/check` principal review (5 engineering fixes).** No spec contradictions surfaced; the deviations recorded in the 2026-06-16 entry all stand. Committed after.
**Context:** A hands-on review pass exercised the vertical against real usage; several rough edges only visible with data in hand, plus a `/check` that interrogated correctness/abstraction.
**UX fixes (from review):**
(1) **Log-symptom form defaults to "+ Create new symptom"** (was: the most-recently-created type) — pre-selecting an existing symptom risks logging an episode under the wrong one; picking an existing one should be deliberate (closer to §6.12's autocomplete intent). With **zero** types the chooser is hidden entirely (a one-option "+ Create new" dropdown is noise) and the name field stands in directly.
(2) **Episode "Linked context" shows siblings only** — the parent SymptomType link was duplicated (header chip + linked context), so a single-episode page read as "Knee pain → Knee pain" (self-link). Parent now lives once, in the header chip (§6.7:1509); Linked context is the §6.7:1533 "related episodes nearby" only, and omits when there are none. **Documented deviation from §6.7:1533** (which lists the parent *in* Linked context) — the header already carries the mandated parent-link, so keeping both was the redundancy.
(3) **Post-hoc vital linking** — `linked_vital_ids` added to the episode PATCH schema/query (scope-checked like create); the "Captured at this episode" section is now edit-aware (+ Link a reading / Remove, replace-whole-array). Closes the asymmetry where `linked_visit_id` was post-hoc-editable but vitals weren't (the *more* demo-critical link). Same-entity editing — NOT the rejected cross-entity creation affordance; readings are still created via `/vitals/new`/Phase-E chat.
(4) **"+ Log episode" from a SymptomType page** passes `?type=<id>`, which **locks** the form's Symptom field (no chooser, no "+ Create new") and returns to that type's page on save — tightens "log an episode FOR this symptom." Bogus/out-of-scope `?type=` falls back to the open chooser.
(5) **Two-page clarity pass** (episode vs type confusion): episode header gets an `EPISODE` tag; the parent chip went verbose→terse (`↑ View symptom →`, matching the timeline link); the type page gained a "This symptom and every episode logged for it" descriptor. Timeline group link renamed `View type →` → **`View symptom →`** (user-facing "symptom," not the internal type/episode split).
**`/check` engineering fixes:**
(1) **`vitalQueries.delete` is now transactional and scrubs the id from episodes' `linked_vital_ids`** (`array_remove`, patient-scoped) — the array is a `uuid[]`, not an FK, so a deleted reading otherwise lingered as a dangling id the symptom serializer emits as `§ unresolved:<id>` in vault context, silently breaking the episode↔reading link the demo relies on. **Demo-relevant correctness fix.** (2) Removed the lone non-null assertion (`data.newType!`) in the episode POST for an explicit `if/else-if/else` guard. (3) **`formatVitalValue` de-duplicated → `lib/vitals.ts`** (single source; `vital-options` re-exports for the client, the serializer imports it — they must agree or the AI cites a value the user can't see). (4) **`NOT_SET` de-duplicated → `lib/select-sentinel.ts`** (condition-options + symptom-options re-export; 0 importer churn). (5) Form rejects `duration < 1` (matches the API's `.positive()`); `siblings()` left deliberately unbounded (the "· N" count needs the full set; trivial at single-patient scale).
**Reasoning:** All five UX fixes sharpened the inverted-one→many model's legibility (the type=symptom-overview vs episode=one-occurrence split is the part users trip on) without touching the schema. The `/check` fixes are pure hardening; #1 is the only one with demo teeth — it keeps the symptom↔vital correlation from rotting on the normal "delete and re-enter a reading" path (§4:433).
**Verified:** `/verify-ui` 18✅ / 6🔍 / 0❌ in a real headless browser (all 8 fixes + timeline/episode/type/form/by-slug/probes); scrub-on-delete proven via API (`['…']` → `[]`); `tsc` + `eslint` + `citation-parser:check` clean; `next build` green; throwaway `ZZZ` data swept, user rows untouched.

**Date:** 2026-06-17
**Decision:** Phase D — **Report vertical** (event entity, §6.6 timeline + §6.7 detail): API · `Log report` form · month-grouped timeline with `All types ▾` filter · detail with `REPORT CONTENT` body + Outcomes + linked-visit context · full inline edit · Delete · live `§ report:` pill. **Source-file upload + PDF preview explicitly deferred to Phase E** (user-scoped this run). The 4th event entity; clones the Visit template wholesale.
**Context:** Report is the simplest-shaped event after Visit — a single narrative body (`content`, markdown) plus two optional FK refs (`linked_visit_id`, `linked_doctor_id`) and a `report_type` enum. Two of its §6.7 features depend on machinery that only lands in Phase E (extraction/upload), so the vertical's scope was set with the user before building: **manual text-entry creation now, file pipeline later.**
**Choice:**
(1) **Source file (`source_file_url`) + PDF preview deferred to Phase E.** §6.12:1860 lists a "Source file" field and §6.7:1534 lists "PDF preview" as the body's alternative — both ride the `/api/files/sign|process` upload pipeline (§9.4:2527-2625), which is Phase E. The form is **text-only** (Content markdown); the detail body renders `content` through react-markdown (the §6.7 "bolded key data within prose" pattern), with a quiet "Edit to add the document text, or upload the file in chat" invitation when empty. Same posture as Visit/Lab/Symptom deferring their `source_report_id` write path to extraction. The schema column already exists (`reports.source_file_url`), so the field surfaces with zero migration when E lands.
(2) **`status` not exposed at direct entry** — the `reports.status` enum (`extracting/ready/failed/committed`) is the Phase 9.4 upload lifecycle. Manual creates land at the DB default **`ready`**; the form never shows it. `extracting/failed/committed` are only reachable through the (Phase E) extraction path.
(3) **Outcomes section built now, empty until Phase E.** §6.7:1534 Report Outcomes = "extracted entities (medications, conditions surfaced from the report)" — queried via `source_report_id` backlinks (`reportQueries.outcomesForReport` → meds + conditions where `source_report_id = report.id`). Those FKs **already exist** on the medication/condition schemas (added in Phase A for 9.4), so the query + section are the correct structure today; they simply render nothing for manually-entered reports until extraction populates `source_report_id`. Section omits-when-empty (Visit precedent), so a hand-entered report shows no empty Outcomes shell. **Built rather than stubbed** — same call as wiring a real (currently-empty) backlink query.
(4) **Linked context = the linked VISIT only; "linked conditions" satisfied by Outcomes — FLAGGED.** §6.7:1534 lists "linked visit, linked conditions" for Report linked context. The Phase 4 schema has **no condition↔report edge** other than `conditions.source_report_id` — which IS the Outcomes section. So the Linked context section surfaces the `linked_visit_id` ("This report came from {visit}", navigable), and conditions-from-this-report appear under Outcomes. The linked **doctor** (`linked_doctor_id`, "Author/source" §4:491) renders in the header **subtitle** ("Apr 12, 2026 · from Dr Sharma"), the §6.7 "capture context merged into subtitle" slot — not in Linked context. **Doc-fix candidate:** §6.7:1534 "linked conditions" is redundant with Outcomes given the schema; either drop it or note the overlap.
(5) **Linked doctor added to the `Log report` form** beyond §6.12:1860's draft (Title · Type · Date · Linked visit · Source file · Content · Notes). The Phase 4 table carries `linked_doctor_id`; surfacing it at entry lets the document's author/source be captured up front. Both linked refs are optional plain Selects of the patient's visits/doctors (the §6.12 rich autocomplete + `+ Create new` modal remains the standing Phase D carryover); both carry the `NOT_SET` "—" clear row. No zero-doctors guard (unlike Visit) — a report requires neither a doctor nor a visit.
(6) **Full inline edit on detail** (no change log — §6.7 Edit corrects the row in place): title · type · date · linked doctor · linked visit (header grid in edit mode) + content + notes (their sections). Both linked FKs are scope-checked on PATCH (`assertLinkedRefsInScope`), mapping an out-of-scope id to a 400 field error (`visit_not_found` / `doctor_not_found`), not a Postgres FK 500 — same guard as Visit's `doctorId`.
(7) **`§ report:<ISO-date>` citation pill** wired as the 11th interactive config (by-slug round-trip on the report date, same collision-suffix limit as visit/lab — a 2nd same-day report cites `report:<date>-2` → resolves null, accepted v1). The serializer already emitted `report:<date>` slugs and the parser already accepted the `report` type — only the pill resolver + `/api/reports/by-slug/[slug]` route were missing. Preview shows title · date · type · "from {doctor}" · extracted-entity count.
(8) **`All types ▾` filter** is enum-backed (the 6 `report_type` values), simpler than the Visit timeline's free-set doctor filter — URL `?type=<enum>`, validated, survives refresh, bogus value falls back to all. No most-recent tint (§6.6:1443 — Visits/Symptoms only) and no outcome badges (Visit-specific, §6.6:1489); the card shows title + type pill + linked visit/doctor context per §6.6:1469. Reused the generic `TimelineShowEarlier` island from the Visit vertical.
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Pull source-file upload into this vertical** — rejected per the agreed scoping; the upload/extraction pipeline is a Phase E surface with its own confirmation UI (§6.11), and bolting a one-off uploader onto the Report form would duplicate work E does properly. (b) **Stub/omit the Outcomes section until E** — rejected; the backlink query is real and correct now, omit-when-empty hides it cleanly, and building it now means E only has to write `source_report_id`, not add UI. (c) **Put linked conditions in Linked context** via a date/name heuristic — rejected; that's synthesis-agent inference (Phase 5), and the only real condition↔report edge is `source_report_id` (→ Outcomes). (d) **Linked doctor in Linked context** rather than subtitle — rejected; §6.7 puts capture context (author/source) in the subtitle, reserving Linked context for forward-links (the visit).
**Reasoning:** Report is the leanest event surface, so the Visit 6.6/6.7 template cloned almost verbatim — the only genuinely new decisions were where the Phase E seam falls (source file, status, Outcomes data) and the linked-conditions interpretation. Drawing the E boundary at "schema columns exist, write path is E" keeps every deferred piece a zero-migration, additive unlock rather than a rework.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean; `citation-parser:check` 11/11. **API smoke (curl, real arogya-dev DB):** create (status→`ready`) · get · by-slug (returns preview shape) · `reportType` filter · PATCH · out-of-scope linked-FK → 400 field error · missing-title → 400 · delete → 204 · 404-after-delete — all correct, `ZZZ` test rows swept. **`verify-ui` (real headless browser) 12✅ / 3🔍 / 0❌:** empty state · form (empty-submit aria-invalid, type/visit/doctor Selects show labels, create+redirect) · month-grouped timeline + subtitle · type filter (narrows + `?type=` + survives refresh + bogus→all) · card→detail nav · detail (title + type pill + "from {doctor}" subtitle + `REPORT CONTENT` markdown bold + no History + Notes + Outcomes-omitted-when-empty) · linked-visit navigation · inline-edit commit-on-blur · bogus-id 404 · by-slug preview · delete→redirect→404; console clean, `ZZZ` data swept. Outcomes render verified out-of-band by seeding a `source_report_id` link directly in the DB (no write API exposes the field): section appears as `+ <med> · medication · View medication →`, then seed rows swept. **Uncommitted — pending user review.**

**Follow-up (2026-06-17, `/check` review — §6.7 vs §9.4 Report-detail contradiction, FLAGGED, deferred to Phase E):** The spec is internally inconsistent about where source-report-derived entities surface on the Report detail and how many kinds: **§6.7:1534** (the detail-surface spec) says *"Outcomes: extracted entities (medications, conditions surfaced from the report)"* — naming exactly two entity kinds, in the **Outcomes** section. **§9.4:2571** (file-handling) says *"the Report's detail page shows **all derived entities** in **Linked Context**,"* and **§9.4:2573** enumerates **seven** `source_report_id`-bearing entities (Medication, Condition, LabReport, VitalReading, SymptomEpisode, Visit, JournalEntry). The Phase D build followed **§6.7** literally: `reportQueries.outcomesForReport` queries **medications + conditions only**, rendered in the **Outcomes** section (not Linked context). Consequence (Phase E only — empty until extraction writes `source_report_id`): a prescription PDF that extraction turns into "3 meds + 1 lab + 1 visit" would surface only the 3 meds on the report detail, dropping the lab + visit. **Decided NOT to broaden now** — building a 7-entity union query for data that's empty until Phase E is speculative, and §6.7 (the section that owns the surface) names exactly two. **Phase-E action when extraction lands:** (a) decide §6.7-Outcomes vs §9.4-Linked-context placement (recommend keeping §6.7's Outcomes, since it owns the wireframe) and **doc-fix the loser**; (b) broaden `outcomesForReport` to all seven `source_report_id` entities so "all derived entities" (§9.4:2571) actually hold — the section + query shape already exist, so it's an additive widen, not a rebuild. This subsumes the "linked conditions redundant with Outcomes" doc-fix noted in the main entry above.

**Date:** 2026-06-17
**Decision:** Phase D — **JournalEntry vertical** (event entity, §6.6 timeline + §6.7 detail): API · `New entry` form · month-grouped timeline (no filter, no tint) · detail (`ENTRY` markdown body + Linked context, NO Outcomes / NO Notes) · full inline edit · Delete · live `§ journal:` pill. The **last event entity** — closes the §6.6/§6.7 event-template family. Cloned from the Report vertical.
**Context:** Journal is the leanest event surface (§6.7:1527 "the body IS the note"), so most of it was a verbatim clone. The genuinely new piece is `linked_entities` (jsonb `{type,id}` refs) which §6.6:1470 + §6.7:1535 render as `§` wiki-citation pills — and which §6.12:1862 marks "AI suggests on save" (Phase E), so they're empty for every manually-entered entry.
**Choice:**
(1) **`linked_entities` excluded from the write schemas; resolver built, empty until Phase E.** Per §6.12:1862 the refs are AI-tagged at write time (onboarding/extraction/synthesis), never set at direct entry — same exclusion as `source_report_id`. Built `journalQueries.resolveLinkedEntities` (batched one query per distinct ref-type → `{label, href}`) covering every entity type that has a detail page (med/condition/doctor/allergy/visit/lab-report/report/symptom/symptom-episode/family-history); **`vital` and unknown types are silently dropped** (no detail page to link to, mirroring the serializer filtering unresolved citations). Renders the `LINKED ENTITIES · N` detail sub-section + the card's bottom pills. Empty for manual entries; **proven to render+navigate** by seeding a `{type:"doctor"}` ref directly in the DB (no write API exposes the column). Same build-it-now-empty-until-E posture as the Report Outcomes section.
(2) **Linked-entity pills are inert on the timeline card, navigable on the detail.** §6.6:1486 wants card pills "clickable → navigate," but the card is a whole-card `<Link>` and nested anchors are invalid HTML — so card pills render as styled spans (the same card-restructure carryover flagged for the Visit/Report timelines, decisions.md), while the §6.7 `LINKED ENTITIES` section renders them as real navigating `Link`s. `JournalLinkedEntityPills` takes an `interactive` prop for the split.
(3) **Slug = `journal:<entryDate>` (date-based), NOT title-based — FLAGGED.** §6.7:1555 says Journal breadcrumb slugs derive "from title" (title-less → `[date]-untitled-N`). But the serializer (`journalEntrySlug`) already commits to `journal:<ISO entry date>`, and the by-slug citation resolver must round-trip the serializer's slug — so the citation slug IS the date, consistent with every other event entity (visit/lab/report/symptom-episode all use the ISO date). The detail route path uses the row uuid; the breadcrumb displays the date. **Doc-fix candidate:** §6.7:1555 "from title" is superseded by the uniform date-slug convention. Same same-day-collision limit as the other events (`journal:<date>-2` → null).
(4) **`mood` added to the `New entry` form** beyond §6.12:1862's draft (Title · Date · Content · Linked entities) — the §4:507 enum column exists and a one-tap mood enriches the personal-journal voice; optional Select, clears to null. Renders as a header pill on the detail + the citation-pill right-note. Same call as the Report form's linked doctor. Flagged.
(5) **NO Outcomes, NO Notes sections** (§6.7:1525/1527 — Journal produces no outcomes; the body is the note). **`(untitled)`** fallback for title-less entries in the detail header, breadcrumb-date, and sibling reference rows (§6.7:1535), with a first-line preview as the reference label. Subtitle voice **"written by you"** (§6.6:1471 / §6.7:1500). **No filter pill** (§6.6:1451), **no most-recent tint** (§6.6:1443). `OTHER JOURNAL ENTRIES FROM THIS MONTH` = `siblingsThisMonth` (same-YYYY-MM, excluding self), with `formatRelativeDate` subtitles ("N days ago" — the app's existing relative form, not §6.7:1535's compact "13d ago").
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **A manual entity-linking UI on the form** — rejected; §6.12 explicitly assigns linking to the AI (Phase E), and a manual multi-entity picker is scope the spec doesn't ask for. (b) **Defer the linked-entity resolver entirely, render refs as inert text** — rejected; the resolver is the correct structure, navigable the moment Phase E writes refs, and proven to work. (c) **Title-based slug per §6.7:1555** — rejected; it would break the serializer round-trip and diverge from the four other event entities' date-slugs. (d) **Drop `mood`** to match §6.12 literally — rejected; the column exists and the field is one low-friction tap that suits the journal's personal register.
**Reasoning:** Journal closes the event-template family with the least new surface area — the only non-mechanical decision was how to handle `linked_entities`, resolved the same way every Phase-E-dependent feature has been (build the real structure, leave it data-starved until extraction/synthesis lands). The vertical feeds the north star: a journal note like "talked to the cardiologist about the dose" becomes citation-addressable (`§ journal:<date>`) and, once Phase E tags it, will surface `§ doctor:` / `§ med:` pills linking the narrative to the structured record — exactly the cross-entity weave the demo moment depends on.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean; `citation-parser:check` 11/11. **API smoke (curl, real DB):** list · create (titled + untitled, mood persists, `linked_entities` null) · empty-content → 400 · bad-mood → 400 · get · by-slug (preview shape) · PATCH · clear-content → 400 (NOT NULL) · delete → 204; `ZZZ` swept. **`verify-ui` (real headless browser) 14✅ / 2🔍 / 0❌:** empty state · form (content aria-invalid, mood Select label, create) · month timeline + "written by you" · title-less card preview · card→detail · detail (title + mood pill + ENTRY markdown bold + NO Outcomes + NO Notes) · OTHER-ENTRIES sibling nav · `(untitled)` header · LINKED-ENTITIES-absent-when-empty · inline-edit commit-on-blur · bogus-id 404 · by-slug preview · delete→redirect→404; console clean, `ZZZ` swept. **Linked-entity resolver proven** by a direct DB seed: `LINKED ENTITIES` section renders, the `§` pill resolves the doctor name + navigates to `/doctors/[id]`. **Uncommitted — pending user review.**
