**Date:** 2026-06-18
**Decision:** Phase D — **Patient profile vertical** (standalone surface §6.10, §6.5 state-detail template variation): `patients.notes` column + migration · `relationship` on the auth stub · `patientQueries.{update,atAGlance}` · singleton `PATCH /api/patient` · `app/patient/[id]/page.tsx` + `components/patient/*` (11 files) — sections IDENTITY → MEDICAL PROFILE → AT A GLANCE → NOTES, full inline edit, disabled `…` menu, Ask AI button. The first standalone (non-entity-CRUD) surface; **CONTACT + primary language deferred** per pre-build scoping with the user.
**Context:** §6.10 is the canonical view of the patient as a *person*, built on the §6.5 detail template with patient-specific variations. It is light/rarely-visited (mostly onboarding edits). Two of its five spec'd sections (CONTACT, plus "primary language" in IDENTITY) have **no backing columns** in the locked Phase-4 `patients` schema, and one section (NOTES) needed a new column — so scope was set with the user before building.
**Choice:**
(1) **`patients.notes` column added** (additive, nullable `text`; migration `0001_tearful_mother_askani.sql`, applied to arogya-dev). §6.10:1689 NOTES is "free-form markdown, user's editorial layer" — no existing column fit. **Explicit sign-off obtained** (schema is locked post-Phase A). Distinct from the **dead** `patients.family_history` text column (§6.10:1660 — family history is its own first-class entity now; that legacy column is never surfaced here). Rendered pre-wrap, no markdown renderer (consistent with every other notes surface in v1).
(2) **CONTACT section + "primary language" omitted entirely** (not stubbed). §6.10:1679 CONTACT (phone/email/emergency contact) and the IDENTITY "primary language" field have no `patients` columns and would each need schema additions; **deferred for this vertical per the user**. The page renders four sections (IDENTITY/MEDICAL PROFILE/AT A GLANCE/NOTES) instead of five. **Flagged:** revisit when contact columns are justified — they're real gaps vs. §6.10, not oversights.
(3) **`relationship` ("Father") lives on the `getCurrentPatient()` stub, not the schema.** §6.10:1671 subtitle is `[age] · [sex] · [relationship] · [location]`. Relationship is a property of the account-holder↔patient *link*, not patient demographics, so it has no home in the locked `patients` schema — it sits in the auth stub alongside `name`/`timezone` (identity-only stub data), a one-place v1.5 swap to the user↔patient join. Read-only in the profile. Location = `city` + `country` → "Pune, India".
(4) **No History section; identity edits aren't versioned** (§6.10:1661). Unlike the state entities, the patient root has **no change log** — every field edits in place via `PatientInlineField` → `PATCH /api/patient`. The PATCH route is a **singleton** (no `[id]`, auth-derived patientId, mirrors `/api/lifestyle`), with no clinical-field refusal map (nothing here is change-logged). Required columns (name/sex/dateOfBirth/country) are `.optional()` but never `.nullable()`; optional columns clear to null.
(5) **IDENTITY mixed-density, but name edited in the header — DEVIATION.** §6.10:1677 lists "primary fields prominent (name, age, sex)". Showing the name a second time (it's already the H1) reads as a bug, so the IDENTITY prominent cards are **age + sex**; name + preferred-name edit in the header (matches how the Medication header owns name editing). Age is **derived** from DOB (`ageInYears`, calendar-correct, added to `lib/datetime.ts`) — display-only; editing DOB in the grid updates the age card on refresh.
(6) **MEDICAL PROFILE weight `history →` link + trend omitted — DEVIATION/GAP.** §6.10:1678/1694 wants weight to link to a weight time-series with an inline trend (`↘ -1.4 kg / 2 mo`). VitalReadings have **no timeline/list page** in v1 (§6.6 lists only five event rails; the vitals vertical is create-form-only — confirmed not over-scope). There is no weight-history surface to link to, so weight edits the `patients.current_weight_kg` snapshot in place; allergies surface as the `N list →` count → Allergies list. **Flagged:** wire the trend/link when a vitals-history surface exists.
(7) **AT A GLANCE "Recent insights" is text-only (no link).** §6.10:1680 lists 8 meta-count rows linking into the wiki; seven destinations exist (conditions/medications/doctors/family-history/allergies/lifestyle/visits) and link live. The **insights feed (§6.8/6.9) is the next Phase D surface and doesn't exist yet**, so that row renders as text to avoid a 404; it becomes a link when the feed lands. (Count reads 0 regardless — generation is Phase E.) Counts via `patientQueries.atAGlance` (one round of `COUNT(*)` — first `count()` use in the codebase; cheaper than loading rows to `.length`); "Recent visits" is the inclusive `[today-30, today]` window so future scheduled visits don't leak in.
(8) **`…` menu (Export record / Archive patient) + photo upload rendered present-but-disabled** (§6.10:1664/1697 sub-text preserved: "full archival PDF", "destructive · confirms first"). Both are post-launch behaviors; the avatar's dashed-ring + camera affordance (§6.10:1693) shows in edit mode with `aria-disabled` ("coming, not broken"). Per the user — keeps the designed surface without dead clicks. **No patient citation pill** (synthesis never emits `§ patient:`/`§ profile:` — the citation-pill `profile` key is the *Lifestyle* payload), so the PATCH route has no GET/by-slug sibling.
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Render NOTES read-only / omit it** rather than add a column — rejected; the user chose the column (cleanest, matches the spec'd editorial layer). (b) **Repurpose `patients.family_history` for notes** — rejected; semantically wrong (it's the dead legacy column) and would muddy the §6.10:1660 "family history is its own entity" decision. (c) **Add `relationship` to the `patients` schema** — rejected; it's a link property, not demographics, and the schema is locked; the stub is the correct v1 home. (d) **Repeat name as a prominent IDENTITY field per §6.10:1677 literal** — rejected (#5); duplicate name on one screen. (e) **Link the insights row anyway** — rejected; ships a known 404 until §6.8 lands.
**Reasoning:** The profile is the patient *hub* (the `/patient/[id]` root was a 404 before this) and a pure §6.5 template variation, so most of it cloned the Medication detail. The only real decisions were where the schema falls short of §6.10 (CONTACT/language/notes/weight-trend) and how to handle the not-yet-built insights link — each resolved to either an explicit deferral (flagged for revisit) or a zero-migration unlock. Feeds the north star indirectly: AT A GLANCE is the navigational spine that lets a reader (or, later, the synthesis agent's surface context) jump from "the person" into any structured record.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean (incl. new files). Migration generated + applied to arogya-dev (`ALTER TABLE patients ADD COLUMN notes text`). **`verify-ui` (real headless browser) 11✅ / 4🔍 / 0❌:** page loads · header (breadcrumb + name + relationship) · subtitle "77 · M · Father · Pune, India" · 4 sections present · NOTES omitted when empty · AT A GLANCE 8 rows + conditions link resolves · insights row text-only (no link) · MEDICAL PROFILE allergies→list link · Ask AI button · `…` Export+Archive present & disabled · Edit mode inline fields + **sex Select shows "Male" not raw `male`** · blank-required-name → field error · preferred-name commit-on-blur persists across refresh · bad numeric weight → 400 field error · bogus patient id → 404. **One ❌ caught + fixed mid-verify:** the sex/blood-type Selects showed the raw enum (`male`) because Base UI's `SelectValue` only resolves labels when `items` is passed to the `Select` root — the older Medication `inline-field` (the copy source) omits it; the newer journal/lab inline-fields pass it. Added `items={options}`. Console clean apart from the expected negative-path 400/404. Throwaway edits restored (preferred name re-cleared to seed null). **Uncommitted — pending user review.**

**Follow-up (2026-06-18, user testing — three refinements):** (1) **Height/weight units inferred from `patients.country`, not hardcoded cm/kg.** The user flagged that cm/kg "assumes outside the US." Fix: `lib/units.ts` — `unitSystemForCountry()` (imperial only for US/Liberia/Myanmar spellings, else metric incl. unknown) + format/parse/edit-seed helpers. The schema stays canonical metric (`height_cm`/`current_weight_kg`); conversion lives only at the input edge in a new `PatientMeasurementField` (shows ft-in/lb for an imperial country, cm/kg for metric, converts to metric before the PATCH). The India demo renders metric — but now *derived* from country (which §4:241 already treats as load-bearing for lab ranges), not assumed. v1 is single-patient so the imperial path isn't browser-reachable; its math is proven by `scripts/check-units.ts` (`npm run units:check`, 25 cases incl. round-trips). Chose inference over a per-field unit toggle (v1.5 polish) or metric-only (the thing the user objected to). (2) **Allergies de-duplicated → MEDICAL PROFILE only.** §6.10 lists allergies in *both* §2 (MEDICAL PROFILE) and §4 (AT A GLANCE); on screen that read as redundant. Kept it with blood type (the safety-critical "emergency card" pair), dropped the AT A GLANCE row. **Doc-fix candidate:** §6.10:1685 — remove the AT A GLANCE allergies row, or note it's redundant with §2. (3) **AT A GLANCE rows enriched with sample names** instead of bare counts (`Conditions · Hypertension, Type 2 Diabetes +2 →`). Stays a plain link list — the names are the §6.5 "light context," NOT the "rich preview cards" §6.5:1382 fenced off (no inline expansion/accordion, which the user also floated but which would make the light profile page heavy). `patientQueries.atAGlance` now returns `{count, names[]}` (top-2, most-recent-first, `+N` overflow) for conditions/medications/doctors/family-history; visits/insights/lifestyle stay plain stat lines; doctor names run through `displayDoctorName` (no "Dr Dr"). **Re-verified:** `tsc`/`eslint`/`units:check` clean; `verify-ui` round 2 = 7✅·2🔍·0❌ (metric display `172 cm`/`70 kg` · allergies in MEDICAL PROFILE + absent from AT A GLANCE · enriched names render + still navigate · weight edit seeds bare `70` + 70→75 persists · garbage height → field error); console clean; seeded height/weight restored to null.

**Follow-up (2026-06-18, `/check` engineering pass — 8 findings, all fixed):** (F1) **`atAGlance` was two sequential `Promise.all` waves** (preview rows, then a second block of 4 `COUNT(*)`) — independent work serialized into a wasted round-trip. Collapsed to **one wave** by folding count+preview into a single query per entity via `COUNT(*) OVER()` (`totalOver`, `.mapWith(Number)` for the bigint→number coercion): 8 queries, one round-trip; `count = rows[0]?.total ?? 0`. (F2) **Page-level `getById` then `atAGlance` were also sequential** though independent → `Promise.all([getById, atAGlance])` (compute the visit window first, notFound-guard after). (F3) **Dead code in `PatientInlineField`** — the `"number"` variant + `heightCm`/`currentWeightKg` `FieldKey`s + the `inputMode` branch went unused once height/weight moved to `PatientMeasurementField`; removed. (F4) **`clearable` was a no-op on the blood-type Select** (selects commit a value, no empty option) → set `clearable={false}` so the prop stops lying; clearing-via-sentinel deferred. (F5) **`ageInYears` used server-local `new Date()`** while the page reasons in patient-tz → signature changed to `ageInYears(dob, today?: string)`, page passes `todayInTimezone(...)` so age flips on the patient's birthday, not the server's. (F6) **Inline-commit engine was duplicated** across `PatientInlineField` + `PatientMeasurementField` (and mirrored the medication field) → extracted `useInlineCommit(endpoint)` owning the PATCH/refresh/pending/error-extraction; both components keep their own draft/seed/validation and call `commit(fieldKey, wireValue)`. Two real callers, not premature. (F7) **AT-A-GLANCE family-history preview showed the raw `relation` enum** (`aunt_uncle`) when `relationSpecific` is null → `relationLabel()` formats it (`Aunt/uncle`) to match the cased free-text it stands in for; kept in the query layer (no UI-layer import) like the existing `displayDoctorName` use. (F8) **Comment drift** in `atAGlance` ("id + name", "one query") corrected to match the rewrite. **Re-verified:** `tsc`/`eslint`/`units:check` clean; `verify-ui` round 1 (base inline-edit through the new hook: blank-required error · preferred-name commit-persist · sex-label · disabled menu · 404) all ✅ + round 2 (units/allergies/enriched names) all ✅; window-total correctness confirmed at the count==limit boundary (2 conditions → both names, no bogus `+N`); consoles clean (only the expected negative-path 404).

**Date:** 2026-06-23
**Decision:** Phase D — **Insights vertical** (feed §6.8 + detail §6.9) + the **Health Wiki rail** (§3 + §6.1:1146). Closes the Phase D entity checklist (Insights was item 13). API: `GET /api/insights`, `GET`+`PATCH /api/insights/[id]` (status-only) · `lib/schemas/api/insight.ts` · `insightQueries.{getById,updateStatus}` · extracted shared `db/queries/entity-links.ts` · feed + detail pages + `components/insights/*` (10 files) · `components/patient/wiki-rail.tsx` + `db/queries/wiki-counts.ts` wired into the patient layout · **`db/seed.ts` expanded** with a demo mini-vault + 5 insights · `INSIGHTS_LIST_SURFACE` + `insightSurfaceContext`.
**Context:** Insights are the first **derived, read-only** surface — AI-generated, immutable (§4:591), no creator form (§6.8:1565). The only mutable axis is the status lifecycle (§4:583). Insight *generation* is Phase E (§5.6); this vertical ships the browse/triage surfaces against seed data. The rail (deferred since Phase A — the layout literally reserved the spot) was added this session at the user's request mid-build.
**Choice:**
(1) **Status-change API is in scope; `PATCH {status}` only, any→any, NO `*_changes` table.** Per the user's pre-build call: "no edit, no form" meant no *creator* form, not read-only-everything — status transitions (Acknowledge / Mark acted on / Dismiss / Restore) are the entire point of the status-grouped feed. Single `PATCH`, status-only, **no transition validation** (the lifecycle has no side-effecting terminal states, unlike medication-discontinue / condition-resolve), so **no `invalid_state_transition` path** — deliberately simpler than the state-entity models, and does NOT contradict the state-transition-error-codes memory (that's for domain state machines with real guards). `dismissedReason` is schema-rejected (`superRefine`) unless `status==dismissed`; cleared otherwise. No `*_changes` audit table — insights are derived AI output, not clinical vault state.
(2) **Demo seed expanded beyond identity-only — APPROVED DEVIATION.** CLAUDE.md Phase-A #5 scopes the seed to identity only (medical data arrives via Phase E onboarding). But §6.8/§6.9 + the user's seed spec require insights with **real `cited_sources`/`linked_entities` ids pointing to live rows** so body citation pills + the Cited-sources section resolve. Per the user's explicit "Expand db/seed.ts" choice, `db/seed.ts` now seeds the §1.3 demo mini-vault (Dr. Anjali Sharma · cardiology; Amlodipine 10 mg + an Apr-3 5→10 mg dose-change; Dizziness type + 4 clustered morning episodes; 4 BP vitals) + 5 insights spanning new/seen/acknowledged/acted_on (0 dismissed). Stable hand-written UUIDs + `onConflictDoNothing` keep it idempotent. Phase E's generator writes real insights into the same table and supersedes these rows; the vault entities remain as demo data. **Run `npm run db:seed` to apply** (left to the user — writes to shared arogya-dev).
(3) **Shared link resolver extracted → `db/queries/entity-links.ts`; journal delegates.** The `{type,id}`→`{label,href}` batch resolver (med/condition/doctor/allergy/visit/lab-report/report/symptom/symptom-episode/family-history; `vital`+unknown dropped — no detail page) lived inside `journal.ts`; Insights is its second consumer (cards, Linked context, Cited-source hrefs), so it moved to a shared module (the same "extract at the 2nd/3rd copy" trigger as `EntityListSections`). `journalQueries.resolveLinkedEntities` now delegates; `ResolvedJournalLink = ResolvedEntityLink` alias keeps journal component imports valid. Added `resolveEntityHrefs` (id→href map) for the Cited-sources section, which renders each source's stored `snippet` and linkifies only resolvable types — so **vital readings show as inert text** (no detail page), gracefully covering §6.9:1627's `VITAL READINGS · N`.
(4) **Feed grouped by STATUS; expand NEW + SEEN — §6.8 "WATCH" reinterpreted (FLAGGED).** §6.8:1563 prose says "NEW and WATCH expanded by default," but `watch` is a **severity** enum value, not a status, in the locked §4 schema — there is no WATCH status bucket. Grouped by the schema status enum (new/seen/acknowledged/acted_on/dismissed) via `EntityListSections`, NEW+SEEN open, the resolved trio collapsed. **Doc-fix candidate:** §6.8:1563 drop the wireframe-era WATCH label (read as SEEN). Two filter pills (status + category, §6.8:1564), URL-param driven. Severity dot at card-left-edge: `urgent`→`bg-destructive`, `attention`→`bg-warning` (both pre-existing tokens — no new color), none for watch/informational (anti-alarm-fatigue §5.6).
(5) **Detail: 3 sections, NOT 4 — Notes omitted (no backing column) — FLAGGED.** §6.9:1608 fixes the order Header→Body→Cited sources→Linked context→**Notes**. The locked §4 `insights` table has **no notes column** (only `dismissed_reason`), so the Notes section has no store — omitted rather than shipping a dead UI or unilaterally adding a locked-schema column. **Needs a call:** add `insights.notes` (sign-off + migration) to honor §6.9:1629, or amend §6.9 to drop Notes. Body renders markdown with **live citation pills** (reuses `remarkCitations`+`CitationPill`, no sparkle envelope). Linked context = **OTHER PATTERNS WITH [ENTITY]**, derived live from other insights overlapping this one's headline entities (via their `linked_entities` OR `cited_sources`; backlinks never stored — Phase 3 tripwire); **RELATED INSIGHTS omitted** (no insight↔insight relation in the v1 schema). Breadcrumb renders truncated UUIDs (no slug column; §6.9:1648 defers slug-gen to Phase 9 — consistent with sibling surfaces).
(6) **Status-action buttons: NO green; periwinkle-primary for the high-value action (§7.3 / user Q3).** Acknowledge / Mark acted on / Dismiss render as prominent top-right buttons (not a `…` menu, §6.9:1607); the current-status button disables; Restore (→`seen`) appears for resolved states. Per Phase 7.3 (green dropped from the system) the §6.9:1647-deferred green tint is **not introduced** — `Mark acted on` (highest-value) gets the periwinkle `default` fill, Acknowledge/Dismiss are neutral `outline`, Restore is `ghost`. Differentiation by label + one primary fill, not color.
(7) **Wiki rail built; Dashboard/Chat/RECENT/search scoped out (no dead links) — FLAGGED.** The rail (§3 three-zone structure) renders: header (branding `arogya v0.1` + patient switcher → profile home) · top-level Insights (live link) + Chat (opens the existing drawer — the §6.2 full-screen surface isn't built) · HEALTH WIKI nine items with live counts (`wikiCounts`, patient-scoped; Symptoms counts types, Labs counts reports) + active-state highlight via `usePathname`. **Omitted:** Dashboard top-level (§6.1 route doesn't exist), RECENT (needs recency tracking not modeled), and the `⌘K · search wiki` footer renders **inert** (wiki search is v1-excluded §10.1) — none are dead links. Counts fetched in the layout on each patient-page navigation (cheap at single-patient scale; keeps the rail a thin client island). **Revisit** the Dashboard/Chat/RECENT items when those surfaces land.
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Separate `seed-demo` script** keeping `db/seed.ts` identity-only — offered; user chose to expand the canonical seed. (b) **In-memory insight fixtures** — rejected (user Q2): real rows exercise the queries/API/status-flow and survive into Phase E. (c) **Duplicate the link resolver into `insight.ts`** rather than extract — rejected; 2nd consumer is the extraction trigger, and Cited-sources needs it too. (d) **Add `insights.notes` now to ship Notes** — rejected; locked schema needs sign-off, deferred to the user. (e) **A new green/`--warning` for the severity dot or acknowledge button** — unnecessary (`--warning` already exists for the amber dot) / rejected (§7.3 drops green). (f) **Map rail "Dashboard" → the profile home** — rejected; the profile (§6.10) is a distinct surface from the dashboard (§6.1), so the label would mislead.
**Reasoning:** Insights is where the wiki stops being a record and starts being the *thinking partner* — the body's live `§` pills + Cited-sources + derived Linked-context make the §1.3 demo moment ("dizziness clusters on mornings after skipped amlodipine… dose raised Apr 3") concretely navigable from a single insight, and the seed encodes exactly that scenario so the surface demonstrates cross-entity reasoning before the generator exists. The rail turns the 13 standalone surfaces into one browsable wiki. Phase E swaps seed insights for generated ones with zero surface change.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean (all new files). **Multi-agent verification workflow** (5 parallel verifiers vs §6.8/§6.9 + CLAUDE.md tripwires → synthesis): **ship-ready, zero must-fix bugs** — 4/5 verifiers `pass` (the rail/cross-cutting verifier ran but didn't emit structured output; rail self-verified via tsc/eslint + route-existence checks). Findings were low-severity nits, all fixed this pass: `dismissedReason` now `superRefine`-rejected off-`dismissed` (was accept-and-drop); stale JSDoc on `resolveEntityHrefs` (`type:id` key); `insight-linked-context` docstring (cited_sources arm); seed INS[4] narrative softened (it predated the seeded vitals — cosmetic); card bottom-row separators normalized. Tripwires independently confirmed: auth via `getCurrentPatient()` (no `request.cookies`), backlinks derived at render, prior insights used for overlap only (no RAG). **Seed NOT yet applied** (needs `npm run db:seed` against arogya-dev) — so the surfaces are unverified against live data; static + type verification only. **No `verify-ui` run this session.** **Uncommitted — pending user review.**

**Follow-up (2026-06-23, user sign-off on the 5 flagged items):** (1) **§6.8 WATCH→SEEN grouping CONFIRMED** — keep NEW+SEEN expanded; §6.8:1563 "WATCH" is a doc-fix (it's a severity, not a status). (2) **Expanded-seed posture CONFIRMED.** (3) **`npm run db:seed` applied to arogya-dev** (idempotent; 1 doctor / 1 med +1 change / 1 symptom type +4 episodes / 4 vitals / 5 insights). (4) **§6.9 Notes section + rail Dashboard/RECENT/search omissions → IMPLEMENT NEXT ROUND**, not dropped. Notes: the spec DOES call for it (§6.9:1629), so next round adds an `insights.notes` column (additive nullable text; user pre-authorized the locked-schema addition via "implement next round") + the 4th detail section. Rail: build the Dashboard (§6.1) + RECENT (§3) items and wire `⌘K` search when those surfaces/recency-tracking exist. (5) **slug-breadcrumb (§6.9:1618) stays auto-deferred to Phase 9** per §6.9:1648 — consistent with every sibling surface, no decision needed. These move from "flagged for sign-off" to a **next-round backlog**; the shipped code is unchanged.

**Follow-up (2026-06-24, `/check` engineering pass — 4 concerns fixed + 1 spec deviation flagged):** (E1) **Feed re-resolved shared refs once per insight** → with all 5 seeded insights linking `amlodipine`, the feed fired 5 identical `medications` queries. Added `resolveEntityRefMap` to `entity-links.ts` (resolves a deduped ref *union* once → `type:id`→link map; `resolveEntityHrefs` now derives from it, DRY); the feed builds the union of all cards' `linked_entities`, resolves once (one query per distinct type for the page), and reads each card's pills back from the map. (E2) **Layout count-freshness comment was wrong** — App Router preserves a shared layout across soft nav, so counts refresh on load + any mutation's `router.refresh()`, NOT on plain `<Link>` sibling nav; comment corrected (no behavior change — fine since every create/delete island calls `refresh()`). (E3) **`wikiCounts` 9-separate-`count()` vs `atAGlance`'s single `COUNT(*) OVER()`** — documented the divergence as intentional (counts-only shape, no preview rows to fold). (E4) **Detail loads the full insight set for live backlinks** — documented the O(insights × refs) in-memory pass as bounded by §5.6's low insight volume. **Re-verified:** `tsc`/`eslint` clean; `verify-ui` 20✅·8🔍·0❌ (feed pills unregressed; statuses restored). **Plus the `/check` ⚠️ spec deviation, fixed same pass:** **vital-triggered insights now keep the §6.9:1610 triggered-by line.** `resolveEntityRefs` drops `vital` (no detail page), so INS[1]/INS[2] (BP-reading-triggered risk/trend) previously rendered `category:` with no "triggered by" — a common case (§5.6 risk flags fire on vitals) and demo-relevant. Fix: the detail page fetches the triggering vital in the same query wave (the trigger type is known before awaiting → no extra round-trip) and renders an **inert** label (`Blood pressure 155/94 mmHg · Jun 14, 2026`, no red-underline accent since it's not navigable) — the same page-less treatment as cited-source vitals, built from the shared `formatVitalValue` + `READING_TYPE_LABEL` (the page may import `components/`; `entity-links.ts` stays db-layer and keeps dropping `vital`). Verified: INS[1] now shows the line, rendered as a non-link span; `verify-ui` 21✅·8🔍·0❌. **Uncommitted — pending review.**

**Follow-up (2026-06-24b, "fix all spec deviations" — 4 fixed, including the deferred Notes; 3 confirmed not-fixable-in-v1):** A spec re-walk of §6.8/§6.9/§3 surfaced two deviations not previously caught, plus a doc-staleness item; the user asked to fix all. **Fixed:** (1) **§6.8:1593 multi-select filter pills** — the feed shipped single-select Selects; spec calls for "multi-select within a pill" (the one surface that warrants it). Rebuilt `insight-filters.tsx` on the checkbox-menu primitive (`DropdownMenuCheckboxItem`; Base UI keeps the menu open on toggle), param is now a comma-list (`?status=new,seen`); the feed parses to a Set and membership-filters. (2) **§6.9:1609 content width** — body column was `max-w-2xl` (672px, under the ~720–800 spec); bumped to `max-w-3xl` (~720px in the px-6 main). (3) **§6.9:1607/1635 "terra/clay"/"warm/terra" action-button tint** — stale doc language (palette reset to periwinkle/stone, terra+green dropped); updated both design.md lines to periwinkle with a supersession note (code already used periwinkle). (4) **§6.9:1629 Notes section — IMPLEMENTED** (was deferred to next round): added the **`insights.notes`** column (additive nullable text; migration `0002_outgoing_gideon.sql`, **applied to arogya-dev**), broadened `insightQueries.updateStatus → update` (status and/or notes), extended the PATCH schema to `{status?, dismissedReason?, notes?}` (`.refine` requires ≥1; superRefine still gates `dismissedReason`; route writes only provided fields, notes/status independent), and added `InsightNotesSection` — a standalone inline editor (always-available textarea, commit-on-blur per CLAUDE.md, empty→null) since the detail has no global Edit mode. Detail now renders all five §6.9:1608 sections (Header→Body→Cited sources→Linked context→Notes). Seed gains a populated note on the acted-on insight (INS[4]) — the original seed spec's "1 ACTED ON insight with a notes field" — set on the live row too. **NOT fixable in v1 (explained, stay deferred):** §6.9:1628 **RELATED INSIGHTS** sub-section — no insight↔insight relation in the v1 schema and no data source until the Phase E generator asserts one (only OTHER PATTERNS, which is derivable, renders); §6.9:1618 **slug breadcrumb** — no slug column, Phase-9 slug-gen, and changing only insights would diverge from every sibling surface; rail **Dashboard (§6.1) / RECENT (§3)** — building those is whole separate surfaces + recency-tracking infra (out of the insights vertical), and **⌘K wiki search is v1-EXCLUDED (§10.1)** so the inert render is correct, not a deviation. **Re-verified:** `tsc`/`eslint` clean; migration applied; API round-trips (notes PATCH persists without touching status; empty PATCH → `validation_failed`; `dismissedReason` off-`dismissed` → `validation_failed`; clear→null); `verify-ui` **23✅·9🔍·0❌** (added multi-select-filter + multi-select-menu + Notes-inline-edit checks; statuses restored; console clean). **Uncommitted — pending review.**

**Date:** 2026-06-24
**Decision:** **Wiki rail sections will expand to show their items in-rail — "all sections, capped" — REOPENING §3:203; build DEFERRED to a future session.** (Insights vertical + rail + all spec-deviation fixes above were committed this session.)
**Context:** The user flagged that the current rail (clean section links + counts → click into a category page → scan cards) is friction against the §3:123 foundational principle ("feels like Obsidian, stored like a database") — a wiki/vault shows items inline, no two-hop tax. But §3:203 explicitly considered and **rejected** a fully-expanded tree for v1 ("~80 items turns the wiki into a file browser… power-user expansion is a v2 question"), so this reopens a locked IA decision.
**Choice:** Per the user's pick, **every** rail section (all nine) expands to a **capped** preview — ~5 most-recent items + a `view all N →` row — not just the named state sections. Collapsed by default; **lazy-load each section's items on expand** (client fetch, most list GETs already exist) so the layout server-render stays light; the section **header still links to the full category page** (keeps the rich list/filter view — expansion is additive, not a replacement). Item labels: names for state entities + symptom types; date/short-label for event timelines (`Apr 30 · Dr. Sharma`).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Named sections only** (Medications/Conditions/Doctors/Family history/Symptoms expand; Visits/Labs/Reports/Journal stay links) — my recommendation, since date-ordered logs read least like wiki pages and a tree of 28 labs is the exact clutter §3 feared; **not chosen** — user wants items visible under every section. (b) **Full uncapped tree** — truest Obsidian but is the §3:203 ~80-item file-browser scenario; rejected. (c) **Keep as-is** (§3's answer: clean rail + Recent + ⌘K) — rejected; doesn't address the friction.
**Reasoning:** The cap (~5 + view-all) is the hinge — it delivers the "see what's under each section without leaving the rail" visibility the user wants while bounding the clutter §3 warned about, so it threads both the foundational principle and the locked rejection. Lazy-loading keeps it cheap. **Build deferred per user** — needs a per-section recent-items query (or reuse list GETs) + a client expand island layered onto the existing `wiki-rail.tsx`. **Doc-fix when built:** §3:203 — note that v1 ships capped in-rail expansion (the v2 "power-user expansion" pulled forward in bounded form).

**Date:** 2026-06-25
**Decision:** **The Dashboard (§6.1) and Full-screen chat + chat-session persistence (§6.2/§6.5) are now explicit, numbered Phase E build items in §10.2 — and Phase E is resequenced into a dependency-aware order.** Per the user: these are load-bearing components, not incidental prerequisites, and the spec shouldn't leave them unscheduled.
**Context:** §10.2's Phase E listed exactly 7 items (extraction · upload · confirmation · onboarding · insight-gen · auto-titling · doctor brief). The dashboard and full-screen chat have full Phase 6 wireframes (§6.1, §6.2/§6.5) but were never assigned a build slot in §10.2 — orphaned surfaces. `docs/progress.md` and the `/phase-e` check had been carrying them informally as "E0a / E0b surface prerequisites" because the listed items depend on them: onboarding exits to the dashboard and the activation banner is its only launcher (§6.3:1246); auto-titling writes `chat_sessions.title` and the doctor brief lives in a chat session (§5.7) — both need persisted, titled, reopenable sessions, which today's ephemeral push-drawer (`components/chat/chat-drawer-provider.tsx`) is not.
**Choice:** Rewrote §10.2's Phase E block to **9 numbered items in recommended build order**: (1) Extraction + router wiring · (2) Upload pipeline · (3) Confirmation surface · (4) Insight generator · (5) **Dashboard shell (§6.1)** · (6) **Full-screen chat + `chat_sessions` (§6.2/§6.5)** · (7) Onboarding · (8) Auto-titling · (9) Doctor brief. Rationale for the order: items 1–4 form a self-contained data-entry loop (extract→confirm→commit→insights) with **no new surface prereq**, and surface the §1.3 north-star earliest because the seed already encodes that vault (so E5/insight-gen validates against real data immediately and supersedes the seed insights). Then each foundational surface is followed by the agents that depend on it — dashboard (5) before onboarding (7); chat sessions (6) before auto-titling (8) and the doctor brief (9). Two doc-internal notes preserved: the dashboard **activation-banner logic stays Phase F** (only the shell is Phase E); the `chat_sessions` (+ messages) table is a **post-Phase-A schema addition needing explicit sign-off before build** (§9.2 lock + CLAUDE.md). Insight-gen moved from old-slot-5 to 4 (right after the confirmation surface whose commit fires its trigger); onboarding moved from old-slot-4 to 7 (forced — it requires the dashboard).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Leave them tracked only in progress.md as derived prerequisites** — rejected by the user; they're real surfaces and belong in the source-of-truth sequencing. (b) **Minimal insert preserving the original 7's order** (drop dashboard before onboarding, chat before auto-titling, no other moves) — rejected; would strand insight-gen after the chat surface and keep onboarding ahead of its own dashboard dependency, i.e. not actually dependency-correct. (c) **Build E0a + E0b first, before any agent** — rejected; items 1–4 have zero surface dependency and deliver the north-star demo soonest, so front-loading the two large surfaces would delay the highest-value loop for no dependency reason.
**Reasoning:** Encoding the two surfaces as numbered slots closes a genuine sequencing gap (the doc previously implied 7 items could ship without two surfaces they structurally require) and makes the build order self-checking against dependencies rather than relying on tribal knowledge in progress.md. Threads the north star: the extract→confirm→commit→insight loop (items 1–4) is exactly the machinery behind the §1.3 demo moment, and ordering it first means that moment is demoable before the heavier onboarding/chat surfaces land.
**Open gate:** the `chat_sessions` schema addition (item 6) is **not yet signed off** — it blocks E0b/E6/E7 but none of items 1–5, so there is runway. Surface for approval before starting item 6.
**Verified:** §10.2 edit applied; no code change this entry. progress.md already carries E0a/E0b as `[ ]` checklist items (reconciled framing — now formal §10.2 slots, not informal prerequisites).

**Date:** 2026-06-27
**Decision:** **Lab Outcomes "View flagged markers" now fulfills the §6.7:1552 highlight behavior (the deferred row-flash is built), and its arrow changes from `→` to `↑`.**
**Context:** User testing the seeded lab (Diabetes/renal/lipid panel) found clicking `View flagged markers →` did nothing and questioned the section's value. Diagnosis: the `<a href="#markers">` anchor + `id="markers"` scroll target both exist and work, but (a) MARKERS sits directly above Outcomes so on a normal-length report the scroll is an invisible no-op, and (b) the spec's documented "briefly highlights flagged rows" (§6.7:1552) was never implemented ("deferred polish") — so the click had zero feedback and felt dead. Separately, the `→` glyph reads as "navigate to another page" when the target is the same-page table above.
**Choice:** Build the flash (user picked this over dropping the link or leaving as-is). On click the link dispatches a `FLASH_MARKERS_EVENT` (`arogya:flash-markers`) window event; `LabMarkersSection` (already a client component) listens and snaps flagged rows to a stronger tint (`bg-warning/30` / `bg-destructive/20`), then fades back over ~1s via a `transition-colors duration-1000` that is present only while not flashing (1200ms timer). Decoupled via a window event so the server-rendered Outcomes section and the client table need no shared parent state (`LabOutcomesSection` became `"use client"` to attach the onClick). Arrow changed to `↑` (`aria-hidden`).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Drop the link, keep the `⚠ N flagged` count** (my initial lean) — the count is a useful severity-colored summary and the markers above already show △ + tint, so the scroll link is low-value on this layout; rejected by user in favor of building the documented behavior. (b) **Leave as-is until Phase E** fills Outcomes with generated insights — rejected; the dead click is a present annoyance.
**Reasoning:** The flash satisfies the spec exactly (highlight, don't filter — normal markers stay visible) and gives the action feedback even when the table is already on screen. The section stays the designated home for Phase-E generated lab insights (warm-tinted), so it isn't just the flagged count long-term. **Deviation logged:** §6.7:1552's literal `→` is now `↑`; design.md:1552 updated to match (same-page jump, not navigation).
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. **Uncommitted — pending review.**

**Date:** 2026-06-28
**Decision:** **Lab marker flag pills now read plain `High` / `Low` / `Critical` — the "Slightly high/low" wording is dropped.**
**Context:** User testing the seeded CKD page asked what the flag pill (`△ Slightly high` on Creatinine) meant — wondered if it referred to dosage. Tracing it surfaced two problems: (1) "Slightly" was injected only at display time by `FLAG_PILL_LABEL` (`components/labs/lab-options.ts`), while the create form's own picker (`FLAG_OPTIONS`) offers plain Normal/Low/High/Critical — so a user selects "High" and the app renders "Slightly high," an input↔output mismatch; (2) clinically, lab flags don't encode magnitude at all. Real labs (HL7 abnormal-flags table 0078) use `H`/`L` for out-of-range and `HH`/`LL` (panic/"critical") for the urgent tier; how-far-out is read from the value vs its reference interval, never a "slightly" qualifier. The app's enum (`normal/low/high/critical`) already mirrors that two-tier model faithfully; only the display wording was off.
**Choice:** `FLAG_PILL_LABEL` → `{ low: "Low", high: "High", critical: "Critical" }`. The value-vs-reference-range (already stored as `value`/`referenceLow`/`referenceHigh` and shown in the MARKERS table) carries magnitude; the amber-vs-red pill color carries the mild-vs-critical tier. Change is in the single shared label map, so it applies everywhere the pill renders (markers table, timeline-card preview, condition linked-context).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Keep "Slightly high/low"** per the §6.7 sketch's literal wording — rejected: no clinical basis, asserts magnitude the 4-value enum doesn't hold, and contradicts the form's labels. (b) **"Above range / Below range / Critical"** — clearer about *what* it compares to, but wordier and non-standard vs the universal H/L; rejected in favor of the conventional plain labels. (c) **Auto-derive the flag from value vs reference range on entry** — clinically how an LIS computes H/L, but ranges/critical thresholds vary by lab/age/sex and reports already carry the flag; left as a separate future feature decision, not bundled here.
**Reasoning:** Plain High/Low/Critical matches clinical convention, matches the form's own input labels (kills the mismatch), and loses no information since degree lives in value+range. **Deviation logged:** supersedes the sketch's literal "SLIGHTLY HIGH" at §6.7:1532; design.md:1532 and the `marker-display.tsx` / `lab-options.ts` comments updated to match.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. **Uncommitted — pending review.**

**Date:** 2026-06-28
**Decision:** **Phase E item E0b shipped — full-screen chat + chat-session persistence — with the `chat_sessions` / `chat_messages` schema addition signed off, auto-titling (E6) folded in, and the ephemeral Ask-AI drawer kept alongside the new surface (not retired).**
**Context:** E0b was the gated item from the 2026-06-25 entry: it needs a post-Phase-A schema addition (`chat_sessions` [+ messages]), which §9.2's schema lock + CLAUDE.md require explicit sign-off for. It also blocks E6 (auto-titling writes `chat_sessions.title`) and E7 (the doctor brief lives in a chat session). Until now the only conversational surface was the ephemeral push-drawer (`components/chat/chat-drawer-provider.tsx`), a ratified 2026-05-30 deviation from §6.2's full-screen spec "until the rail Chat surface arrives." The Phase B/C plumbing (useChat, `/api/chat` streaming synthesis, `chatRequestSchema`, `AiMessage` + citation pills + `tokenizeCitations`) was all in place; only persistence + the surface were missing. D2 had also left the §6.2:1192 grounding header and §6.2:1207 `grounded in →` per-message footer open.
**Choice:**
- **Schema (signed off):** two infrastructure tables, not part of the locked 14 (sibling to `extraction_sessions`). `chat_sessions` (id, patient_id cascade, nullable title, created_at, updated_at) + `chat_messages` (id, session_id cascade, role enum user/assistant, text content, created_at). Migration `0003_awesome_dust.sql`, applied to arogya-dev. Indexes: sessions by (patient_id, updated_at desc) for the history list; messages by (session_id, created_at) for transcript load.
- **Message shape: role + text `content`** (not jsonb parts). Synthesis is text-only markdown with citation glyphs inline, so a text column round-trips perfectly; reconstructed as a single text UIMessage part on load. If E1 inline log-confirmations later need structured parts, add a nullable jsonb `parts` column then (additive).
- **Grounding is derived, never stored** (`lib/citations/grounding.ts` tokenizes assistant text) — same contract as wiki backlinks. Closes the two D2 chat deferrals: conversation header `grounded in: [entities] · N sources` (aggregated, live) + per-message `grounded in →` footer, both reusing `CitationPill`.
- **Auto-titling (E6) folded in:** `lib/agents/auto-titling.ts` (Haiku 4.5, non-streaming, bare-string output sanitized — no Zod, nothing structured to validate). Fired from `/api/chat`'s synthesis `onFinish` on the first exchange (incoming length 1 + null title), best-effort/swallowed on failure; `chatQueries.setTitleIfNull` guards against clobbering a manual rename. Header falls back to the first user message while title is null.
- **Drawer kept, not retired** (user's call): rail "Chat" item now links to `/patient/[id]/chat` (full-screen, persisted); the floating per-entity-page "Ask AI" button keeps opening the ephemeral drawer (context stays visible, thread survives navigation). Two conversation models coexist.
- **Surface:** `/patient/[id]/chat` (new draft, sessionId null) + `/patient/[id]/chat/[sessionId]` (persisted). Three-column = rail (layout) · `ChatHistoryList` (collapsible CHATS) · `ChatConversation` pane. Session menu (rename inline / delete) in the conversation header per §6.2:1193. Lazy session creation on first send (no empty-orphan rows); URL swapped via `history.replaceState` so the in-flight stream isn't remounted; real navigation remounts the surface via a route-keyed `key`.
**Alternatives considered:** (a) **Retire the drawer → Ask AI opens a full-screen session** (matches §6.4:1354 literally) — rejected by the user; the drawer's page-stays-visible value holds for quick entity-page questions, and my "Ask-AI brief threads vanish" objection doesn't bite because the brief capability (E7) is invoked only from full-screen (§6.2:1182) and the drawer's starters already omit it. Keeping the drawer introduces no *new* deviation — it declines to close an already-ratified one. (b) **jsonb `parts` message column** — forward-compatible with structured parts but overkill while synthesis is text-only; deferred to an additive migration if/when E1 needs it. (c) **Defer auto-titling to a separate E6 pass** with a truncated-first-message fallback — rejected; titles are what make the CHATS list usable and the wiring was already in place. (d) **Store grounding on the message row** — rejected; violates the backlinks-derived-at-render tripwire and would drift from the inline pills.
**Reasoning:** Closes the gated E0b item and unblocks E6 (done here) + E7. The derive-don't-store grounding choice keeps a single source of truth (the message text) for both the inline pills and the trust strips. The drawer/full-screen split respects the prior ratified decision rather than reversing it, and keeps each affordance's value prop intact. Lazy creation + replaceState avoids both empty-session litter and mid-stream remounts.
**Verified:** `chat_sessions:check` smoke green (create · patient-scoped get · ordered messages · setTitleIfNull-sets-once-never-clobbers · rename · list · delete-cascades-messages, all against arogya-dev). `tsc` + full `eslint` clean. `next build` compiles both chat routes + the session API routes (no RSC/boundary errors). Migration applied. **Uncommitted — pending review.** Not yet exercised end-to-end in the browser (streaming persistence + auto-title round-trip needs a live API key + manual run).

**Date:** 2026-06-29
**Decision:** **Three E0b chat refinements from first browser testing: (1) dropped the conversation-level "grounded in:" header line; (2) made `§ lab-result:` citations clickable (they resolve to the parent lab report); (3) reworded the empty-state copy to name the patient and state the full-vault promise.**
**Context:** User drove the new full-screen chat (E0b) and flagged: (a) "grounded in:" appeared twice — once in the conversation header (§6.2:1192) and once per-message (§6.2:1207) — and the header line wrapped across, eating a chunk of the chat width; (b) lab references in the grounding strip weren't clickable while every other type was; (c) the empty-state line "Ask anything about this record…" reads as deictic ("the page in front of you"), which is wrong on the full-screen chat where no entity is in focus, and undersells that synthesis always sees the entire vault (no RAG — architectural rule).
**Choice:**
- **(1) Header grounding removed.** Kept only the per-message `grounded in →` footer (§6.2:1207). The header retains title + … menu. **Deviation from §6.2:1192** (which puts a grounding line under the title): it duplicated the footer and cost horizontal space for no added signal. Logged; design.md not yet amended.
- **(2) lab-result citations made interactive.** Root cause (verified by tokenizing the real forms): the synthesis prompt (10.3:64) tells the model to cite markers as `§ lab-result:creatinine` and the serializer emits `lab-result:` slugs, but `INTERACTIVE_ENTITY_CONFIGS` had no `lab-result` entry, so the pill fell through to an inert span. Lab results have no detail page of their own, so the new config resolves the marker to its **parent §6.7 lab report** via a new `labResultQueries.byMarkerSlug` (match `slugify(markerNormalized ?? marker)`, most-recent on tie — the model usually cites the short no-date form) + endpoint `GET /api/lab-results/by-marker/[slug]`; the popover shows the measured value + flag and `View full →` lands on `/labs/[reportId]`. **Audit of all citation types:** every other vault type (med, condition, doctor, allergy, family-history, lifestyle, visit, lab-report, symptom, symptom-episode, report, journal) is already interactive; the only remaining inert vault type is **`vital`**, which is by-design — vital readings are create-only with no detail page (the prompt cites `§ vital:bp` but there's nowhere to land). External `↗` pills stay inert by design. **Known cosmetic edge:** the full serializer slug `lab-result:creatinine:2026-04-12` tokenizes to a `creatinine` pill plus dangling `:2026-04-12` text (the load-bearing parser's slug grammar excludes `:`); the resolver keys on the marker so the pill still resolves — left as-is to avoid touching the Phase-B parser + its round-trip smoke test.
- **(3) empty-state copy** → "Ask me anything about {firstName}'s health — I read the whole record before answering, so you don't have to find the right page first." Names the patient (7.1 voice: never "the patient"), states the full-vault trust promise, and directly answers "what if I'm not on a specific page." First name threaded server-side through the pages → FullScreenChat → ChatConversation. The ephemeral Ask-AI drawer keeps its "this record" copy — it IS opened from a specific entity page (surface-context-scoped), so the deixis is apt there.
**Alternatives considered:** (1) keep the header line but make it collapse/truncate — rejected; it's redundant with the footer, simplest to drop. (2) leave `lab-result` inert like `vital` — rejected; lab markers are the §1.3 north-star payload ("creatinine at 1.4"), high-value to click through; vs. extend the tokenizer to carry the marker+date slug — deferred (load-bearing parser, separate change). (3) generic "Ask anything about the record" (drop only "this") — workable but colder; naming the patient is warmer and on-voice.
**Verified:** `byMarkerSlug` resolves seeded `creatinine → "Creatinine 1.5"` in the renal panel report; unknown marker → null. `tsc` + `eslint` clean on all touched files. Browser re-test of the rendered pills/popovers still pending a live run.

**Date:** 2026-06-29
**Decision:** **E0b spec-compliance pass: amended design.md:1192 to ratify the dropped conversation-header grounding line, and built the two remaining §6.2 input-surface elements — bottom-strip shortcuts (6.2:1200) and post-first-reply contextual follow-ups (6.2:1199).**
**Context:** The `/check` review flagged four §6.2 deviations in the shipped E0b chat. Three are addressed here (the fourth — session-menu "Export", 6.2:1193 — is intentionally deferred to E7, where the PDF/transcript export lands).
**Choice:**
- **design.md:1192 amended.** The "Conversation header surfaces grounding" locked decision now reads as title + `…` menu only, with an inline note that the `grounded in:` header line was dropped 2026-06-29 (duplicated the per-message footer, ate header width) and grounding lives solely in the 6.2:1207 footer. Keeps the doc as current truth without erasing the original intent.
- **Bottom-strip shortcuts (6.2:1200)** — a persistent, conversation-state-independent utility row above the input: `Prep next visit` / `Med interactions` / `Trend in 90d`, each submitting a fixed prompt through the same path as typed input. Styled quieter than the empty-state starters (small stone chips) so it reads as utility, not primary suggestions.
- **Contextual follow-ups (6.2:1199)** — 2-3 chips shown only in the lull after the first AI reply (`assistantCount === 1 && status === "ready"`), gone once the next turn starts ("most messages have no chips"). **Interpretation flagged:** v1 uses a *fixed, generally-useful* set (`What should I raise with the doctor?` / `Has anything changed recently?` / `What else should I keep an eye on?`) rather than reply-derived suggestions — truly contextual generation would need an extra model call per response, which is out of E0b scope and not a listed agent. The fixed set satisfies the spec's letter (sparse, post-first-reply, 2-3 follow-ups) and on-voice intent (questions-to-raise / change / care-gaps).
**Alternatives considered:** (a) generate contextual follow-ups via a small Haiku call on the first reply — richer, but adds a model call + latency to every first turn and a new agent surface; deferred as a possible v1.5 upgrade. (b) Fold shortcuts into the empty-state starters — rejected; 6.2:1200 explicitly wants them *independent of conversation state* (always visible), which the starters (empty-state only) are not.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. Rendered behavior (chip visibility window, shortcut submission) pending the same live browser pass as the rest of E0b.

**Date:** 2026-06-29
**Decision:** **Resolved the `/check` principal-review engineering findings on E0b chat persistence — moved turn persistence + auto-titling off the response critical path, made the turn atomic, hardened message scoping/ordering, and trimmed list refetches.**
**Context:** The review surfaced seven concerns. All addressed (the bigger three were interrelated, so the persistence flow was reworked as a unit).
**Choice:**
- **(1) Auto-title off the critical path.** Titling previously ran `await`ed inside the synthesis `onFinish`, holding the UI stream open ~1s past the visible text (prolonged "streaming" state) with no timeout (hang risk). Now the route captures the assistant text in `onFinish` and does all post-stream work — persist + title — in a Next 16 `after()` callback, which keeps the function alive past response flush without blocking it.
- **(2) Atomic turn / no orphans.** The user message was persisted *before* synthesis ran, so a failed generation left an orphan user row and a retry double-wrote it. Now both rows are written together in `after()`, gated on a non-empty assistant reply — a failed/empty turn persists nothing.
- **(3) Deterministic ordering.** `getMessages` orders by `createdAt asc` plus a `case role='user' then 0 else 1` tiebreak, so a turn renders correctly even if the two rows' timestamps collide. (Primary ordering is still the distinct-transaction createdAt; the tiebreak is belt-and-suspenders.)
- **(4) Self-scoping reads.** `getMessages(patientId, sessionId)` now inner-joins `chat_sessions` on `patient_id`, so a foreign/stale session returns `[]` on its own — no longer reliant on the caller having checked `getSession`. Callers + smoke test updated; the smoke test now asserts the cross-patient empty result.
- **(5) Fewer refetches.** The CHATS list previously refetched on every assistant finish. Now it refetches only when it must: on session creation (new row appears) and once ~2s after a *fresh* conversation's first reply (to catch the `after()`-set auto-title). Reopened/continued conversations don't refetch on finish; navigation re-reads server-side. A one-shot timer (cleaned up on unmount) replaces per-turn polling.
- **(6) Removed the redundant inner `key`** on `ChatConversation` — the page already keys the whole `FullScreenChat` on the route session id, so real navigation remounts; lazy-create deliberately doesn't.
- **(7) Empty-name guard** — the empty-state copy's first-name derivation falls back to "your family member" if a patient ever has a blank name (can't happen with the stub, but the copy no longer renders "'s health").
**Alternatives considered:** (1) bound titling with `AbortSignal.timeout` and keep the await — rejected; still prolongs the stream and only caps the worst case. Trade-off accepted for `after()`: the auto-title now lands in the sidebar a beat after the first reply (the #5 delayed refetch), not instantly — fine, the session shows its fallback (first user message) until then. (3) add a monotonic `seq` column — rejected; needs a migration + sign-off, and the role tiebreak gives the same guarantee with no schema change. (4) leave `getMessages` caller-scoped with a doc note — rejected; defense-in-depth at the query is cheap and removes the footgun.
**Known residual:** a fresh conversation whose *first* reply fails still leaves an empty session row (created eagerly on first send) in the list — same class as before, now zero-message instead of one-user-message. Low priority; a future sweep can prune empty sessions.
**Verified:** `chat-queries:check` green incl. the new patient-scope assertion. `tsc` + `eslint` clean. `next build` compiles (the `after()` import + all chat/lab-results routes). Browser pass still pending.

**Date:** 2026-06-29
**Decision:** **E1 (start) — locked the extraction output schema and built the extraction agent. (a) Extended `ambiguities` from `string[]` to `{field,question,options}[]`; (b) extraction's match context is a new `buildMatchingDictionary` (names + UUIDs, no change logs) rather than `buildVaultContext(...,{includeInsights:"none"})`; (c) router→chat wiring deferred to land with E3.**
**Context:** Starting Phase E build order at E1 (extraction agent + router wiring). Two locked-decision touchpoints surfaced before coding; both signed off.
**Choice:**
- **(a) Structured `ambiguities` — signed off.** The §5.4 output example showed flat `string[]`, but §6.11's confirmation UI (E3) renders ambiguity prompts as inline-resolve chips with selectable options that block Confirm until answered — flat strings can't drive that. Extended the Zod entity schema's `ambiguities` to `z.array({field, question, options})`. Kept the §5.4 field names (`intent`/`target_entity_type`/`matched_entity_id`/`extracted_data`/`source_excerpt`) — they were already wired through Phase B. **Doc-fixed both sections to agree:** §5.4 example now shows the structured object; §10.3:3203 output-format bullets renamed from the divergent `type`/`bucket`/`extracted_fields`/`existing_entity_match` to the §5.4 canonical names (+ added `source_excerpt`).
- **(b) Matching dictionary, not full vault — deviation from the §10.2/phase-e checklist phrasing, faithful to §5.4.** The checklist says extraction uses `buildVaultContext(...,{includeInsights:"none"})`. But that serializer emits **slug** citations (`§ med:amlodipine`), not UUIDs, so the agent literally cannot fill the locked `matched_entity_id: uuid()`. And §5.4:757 prescriptively specs the matching dictionary as "names + IDs only, **no change logs**," which the full insights-off vault is not. Added `buildMatchingDictionary(patientId)` **in the canonical `vault-context.ts` module, reusing the same `*Queries` layer** (not a raw DB read — honors the "go through the canonical layer" tripwire). Emits the four match-or-create state entities (medications/conditions/doctors/allergies) as `name … (id: <uuid>)`, no change logs, no event/time-series entities (those are always create-new per Phase 3, nothing to match). Empty vault → an explicit "treat every extraction as new" line.
- **(c) Router→chat wiring deferred to E3.** The router's `log` branch must land on the E3 confirmation surface (`/patient/[id]/extract/[sessionId]`), which doesn't exist yet — wiring it now dead-ends at a 404. Built the extraction agent (the foundational unit both the E2-upload path and the quick-log path consume) this session; the router-into-chat layer (classify → question/log/ambiguous branches + inline disambiguator) lands with E3 when there's a real destination. Surfaced sequencing call, not a silent drop of the D3 carryover.
**Built:** `lib/agents/extraction.ts` (`runExtraction`, Sonnet 4.6, text + vision [base64 or URL image source], `EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT` composed from §5.4 + §10.3, three-bucket new-vs-update, never-fabricate, preserve source language, empty-array on unreadable source, AgentError-wrapped, JSON-fence-stripped, Zod-validated — patterned on `router.ts`). `buildMatchingDictionary` in `_shared/vault-context.ts`. Schema change in `_shared/schemas.ts`.
**Alternatives considered:** (b) have the agent return the slug it saw and resolve slug→UUID server-side like citation pills — rejected; would re-open the just-locked `matched_entity_id: uuid()` (→ string), and slugs collide (the `-2/-3` suffix v1.5 issue) so they're an unstable match key. vs. adding UUIDs into `buildVaultContext` output — rejected; pollutes the synthesis context (the canonical consumer) with ids it never cites. (c) wire the router now with the `log` branch redirecting to a stub/404 confirmation route — rejected; dead-end UX, and the wiring is small once E3 exists.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean on all touched files. Agent not yet exercised against a live model (no E2/E3 caller yet) — first real run lands with the upload pipeline.

**Date:** 2026-06-29
**Decision:** **E2 — file upload pipeline (§9.4) built: two endpoints + storage helpers + extraction-session queries + pipeline lib + FilePreview. Extended the extraction agent with a PDF/`document` source; locked the upload→Report→session status mapping; added a server-side path-scope trust boundary.**
**Context:** Phase E build order step E2, giving the E1 extraction agent its first real (file) caller. The browser-drivable end (`/extract/[sessionId]`) is E3, so E2 is validated by a round-trip check script, not UI.
**Choice:**
- **Two endpoints, thin over a lib.** `POST /api/files/sign` → `createSignedUploadUrl` (browser PUTs direct to Supabase, no server round-trip, 9.4:2528). `POST /api/files/process` → `processUpload()` in `lib/files/pipeline.ts` (extracted so it's unit-testable without the dev server). `maxDuration=60` on /process — extraction runs synchronously in-request (no job queue, §9.3 tripwire); a Sonnet PDF pass is ~6s.
- **PDF support added to the agent (E1 extension).** `ExtractionSource` gained `document` / `document_url` variants → Anthropic native document block (base64 / URL). Lab reports + discharge summaries are PDFs; the pipeline must send what the agent accepts. Images still go through the vision block. HEIC excluded from accepted uploads (Anthropic image source doesn't take it; 9.4:2614 names it for display only) — `ACCEPTED_UPLOAD_MIME_TYPES` is the single source of truth, shared by the sign schema.
- **Status mapping (consistent with `buildVaultContext`'s `ready|committed` filter).** Report created `extracting` up-front (9.4:2601, file survives failure) + session `pending`. Non-empty extractions → session `ready_for_confirmation`, report **stays `extracting`** (NOT vault-visible until its entities are committed at E3 → `committed`). Empty array (couldn't read, 5.4:814) OR a thrown AgentError → session `failed`, report `failed`. Extraction failure is never a 5xx — `processUpload` returns the ids so the client still lands on the §6.11 failure state; only DB/storage errors propagate to 500. Added `reportQueries.setStatus` (status is pipeline-managed, deliberately outside the editable `ReportUpdate` set).
- **Path-scope trust boundary.** `/process` takes a client-supplied `path`; `processUpload` rejects (`path_out_of_scope` → 400) any path not under `patients/{authPatientId}/uploads/`. patientId is always auth-derived; the path prefix is re-derived from it, so a client can't name another patient's object. This is the E2 analog of E3's prompt-injection concern.
- **FilePreview** (`components/reports/file-preview.tsx`): image → lightbox (Esc/click close, body-scroll lock); PDF → `<iframe>` + "Open full PDF →"; else download link. **Deviations noted in-file:** plain `<img>` not `next/image` (signed, short-lived, host-dynamic URLs would need remotePatterns config + would cache an expiring URL); multi-page `1 of N` nav (9.4:2615) deferred (browser PDF viewer pages natively; react-pdf counter is later polish). No caller yet — E3 + §6.7 Report detail consume it.
**Built:** `lib/storage.ts` (+`buildUploadPath`/`uploadPathPrefix`/`createSignedUploadUrl`/`downloadFile`), `db/queries/extraction-session.ts` (+barrel), `lib/files/pipeline.ts`, `lib/schemas/api/files.ts`, `app/api/files/{sign,process}/route.ts`, `components/reports/file-preview.tsx`, `db/queries/report.ts` (+`setStatus`), `lib/agents/extraction.ts` (PDF source). Test: `scripts/check-file-pipeline.ts` + `npm run file-pipeline:check`.
**Alternatives considered:** (a) process the upload through our server (multipart) instead of signed direct PUT — rejected; 9.4 explicitly avoids the round-trip. (b) move report to `ready` on extraction success — rejected; `ready` is vault-visible and the entities aren't committed until E3, so it'd leak uncommitted uploads into synthesis context. (c) background/queued extraction — rejected; §9.3 tripwire forbids a job queue in v1, so it's a synchronous in-request call with raised maxDuration.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean on all touched files. `file-pipeline:check` green end-to-end: Tier 1 (path helpers + out-of-scope rejection); Tier 2 live round-trip — minimal text PDF → signed PUT (200) → process (6.3s) → session `ready_for_confirmation`, report `extracting`, extracted Telmisartan 80mg matched to the seeded med (`update`, correct UUID), cleaned up. **Known residual:** an abandoned confirmation leaves an `extracting` report (same class as E0b's empty-session residual) — prune in a future sweep.

**Date:** 2026-06-29
**Decision:** **Resolved the six `/check` engineering concerns on E1+E2 before checkpoint.**
**Context:** Spec-compliance + principal review of E1/E2 found no blockers but six concerns worth fixing while the code was fresh. All addressed.
**Choice:**
- **(1) mimeType derivation.** Reports store no MIME column but §9.4 FilePreview needs one. Added `lib/files/mime.ts` `mimeFromPath()` (extension → MIME; `buildUploadPath` preserves the extension). E3 + §6.7 Report detail derive FilePreview's `mimeType` from `sourceFileUrl` — no schema change.
- **(2) Single source of patient identity.** `processUpload` no longer calls `getCurrentPatient()` internally; `timezone` is passed in by the route (which already resolved the CurrentPatient). The lib now takes all identity by argument — one source of truth, no ambient auth call, trivially testable.
- **(3) Trust stored content type.** Pipeline prefers `downloadFile`'s `contentType` over the client-declared `mimeType` when it's an accepted type — defends against a mislabelled upload (PNG-declared PDF) going down the wrong vision/document path.
- **(4) Atomic outcome write.** Replaced the two non-transactional writes (session `setOutcome` + report `setStatus`) with one transactional `extractionSessionQueries.recordOutcome(...)` (`db.transaction`, per the medication-change-log precedent) — session + report status can't diverge. `reportStatus` set only on failure (success leaves report `extracting` → `committed` at E3).
- **(5) Upload size cap.** `MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES` (25 MB) enforced at /process after download (fails before spending extraction tokens), plus an advisory client-reported `sizeBytes` in the /sign schema for early rejection. Noted: the Supabase bucket file-size limit is the hard backstop; the client value isn't a trust boundary.
- **(6) Re-validate stored jsonb.** Added `parseStoredExtractionOutput()` (safeParse → null) in `lib/agents/extraction.ts` and a doc note on `extractionSessionQueries.getById` — E3 must validate the untyped `extraction_output_json` on read, never cast; a drifted/hand-edited row degrades to the failure state, not a crash.
- **Dead-code removal.** Dropped the now-uncalled `reportQueries.setStatus` (the pipeline uses `recordOutcome`); E3 reintroduces a report-status write with its real commit caller.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. `file-pipeline:check` green end-to-end (Tier 1 + Tier 2 live PDF round-trip; this run matched both the seeded medication AND doctor by UUID). `extraction:check` unaffected.

**Date:** 2026-06-30
**Decision:** **Pulled the E1 router→chat wiring forward (was deferred to E3): the Haiku router now runs on every typed full-screen-chat input, branching question/log/ambiguous. `log` lands on a STUB §6.11 confirmation page until E3.**
**Context:** E1 was marked 🔶 because the agent shipped but the D3 carryover (router-on-every-input) wasn't wired — `/api/chat` was synthesis-only. User asked to complete it now with the `log` branch pointed at a stub destination.
**Choice:**
- **Client-side classify-then-branch** (not a server rewrite of `/api/chat`). The chat client calls `POST /api/chat/classify` (Haiku `runRouter`) on typed input, then branches: `question` → the existing synthesis stream (untouched — keeps E0b persistence/`after()` intact); `log` → `POST /api/chat/quick-log` → navigate to the confirmation page; `ambiguous` → inline 2-button disambiguator ("Log it" / "Ask about it"). Chosen over server-side single-call because the streaming response shape (`useChat`/`toUIMessageStreamResponse`) doesn't cleanly carry a non-stream branch signal, and client orchestration is lower-risk + explicitly testable. The router sees no vault (§5.5:827), so client-side classification leaks nothing.
- **Canned chips bypass the router.** Starters/follow-ups/shortcuts are definitionally questions → call `runQuestion` directly, skipping a needless Haiku round-trip. Only free-typed input is classified.
- **Classifier failure biases to `question`** (§5.5 — wrong-route-to-chat is the cheap failure); the classify route also degrades to `{intent:"question"}` on `runRouter` error rather than blocking input.
- **`log` is not a chat turn** — `processQuickLog` (text counterpart of `processUpload`, in `lib/chat/quick-log.ts`) stores the typed text on a placeholder Report's `content` (the §6.11 "Source · text input"), creates the extraction_session, runs extraction, records the outcome. The user navigates to the confirmation screen; nothing persists to `chat_sessions`. **Deviation noted:** §6.2:1197's "logging returns inline confirmation and the conversation continues" is the post-commit refinement — deferred to E3; for now the canonical §5.5/§6.11 flow (log → confirmation screen) wins.
- **Stub confirmation page** `app/patient/[id]/extract/[sessionId]/page.tsx` — reads the session (`parseStoredExtractionOutput`), renders source (text block or `FilePreview` via `mimeFromPath`) + the parsed extractions + a failure state. Clearly labelled a stub; E3 replaces it with the §6.11 split-panel (ambiguity chips, new-vs-update toggles, per-card commit). This finally gives `FilePreview` its first caller.
- **Scope: full-screen chat only.** The ephemeral Ask-AI drawer is NOT wired (logging from a side-panel that can't navigate is its own UX question) — noted as a follow-up.
- **Compound input** ("BP 152/95 — is that concerning?") routes as `log` (router returns log; extraction does the counting — verified: yields a med update + a vital create). The §5.5 post-confirm synthesis-forward is deferred to E3 (needs the commit step).
**Built:** `app/api/chat/classify/route.ts`, `app/api/chat/quick-log/route.ts`, `lib/chat/quick-log.ts`, the stub `extract/[sessionId]/page.tsx`, classify/quick-log Zod schemas, and the `chat-conversation.tsx` wiring (`ensureSession`/`runQuestion`/`runLog`/router-aware `submit` + inline disambiguator). Test: `scripts/check-quick-log.ts` + `npm run quick-log:check`.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean; `next build` green (both new routes + the extract page compile). `quick-log:check` green — compound input → Telma→seeded-Telmisartan med `update` (correct UUID) + BP `vital_reading` create. Browser pass of the live classify→branch + disambiguator + stub page pending a `verify-ui` run.

**Date:** 2026-07-01
**Decision:** **Built E3 — the §6.11 extraction confirmation surface + the human-in-the-loop commit engine (the only path extraction output becomes vault rows). CREATE for all 8 entity types; UPDATE for the 4 state types via their change-log/PATCH helpers.**
**Context:** E1 (agent + router→chat wiring) + E2 (upload pipeline) shipped; extraction ran end-to-end but dead-ended at a read-only STUB confirmation page — nothing could reach the vault. E3 replaces the stub with the real split-panel surface and adds the commit endpoint. User sign-off (this session): commit ALL 8 types; "Edit manually instead" PREFILLS the §6.12 /new form.
**Choice:**
- **Commit engine `lib/extraction/commit.ts` (`server-only`).** Per-card mapper: extraction snake_case (§5.4:763) → typed insert, re-validated against real column/enum constraints. `commitCard(ctx, card)` returns a per-card result; a *blocked* commit (missing required field / unmatchable ref / no-op update) resolves `{ok:false, error}` with voice-compliant copy shown on the card, not a request error.
- **Never-fabricate mapping (§5.4:794).** Unextracted field → null; unrecognised enum value → dropped (checked against `pgEnum.enumValues`), never guessed. Documented required-field defaults where the agent structurally never emits the field: medication `category`→`allopathic`, allergy `category`→`other` (NOT NULL, no DB default). Composite splits: BP `"152/95"`→primary/secondary; lab `reference_range` `"70-100"`/`"<100"`/`">5"`→low/high. Free-text refs that can't become a UUID FK are preserved in `notes`, not dropped: medication `purpose`, lab `ordering_doctor`.
- **Entity-type write model (§5.4:791) simplifies UPDATE scope.** Event entities (lab/vital/visit/symptom) are ALWAYS create-new — an `update` mode on one is coerced to create server-side. Only the 4 state entities support UPDATE, applied through their existing change-log + PATCH helpers (diff extracted vs current → append `*_changes` row for a changed change-logged field; PATCH plain fields) so the change-log tripwire is never bypassed. medication dose/frequency is the §1.3 north-star case ("cardiologist raised the dose").
- **Source links.** Committed entities carry `source_report_id` for the 7 schema-carrying types; doctor + allergy have no such column (Phase A) → commit without a backlink (noted). **Schema-query extension (additive, not a new column):** `symptomEpisodeQueries.create`'s `NewEpisodeInput` gained optional `sourceReportId`/`recordedBy` + the insert now stamps them — the column already existed; the helper just didn't expose it. No migration.
- **Visit doctor + symptom type resolution.** A visit's doctor is NOT NULL and the agent gives only a name → match an existing in-scope doctor by case-insensitive name; NO match blocks the card ("add the doctor first") rather than fabricating one with an invented specialty. Symptom episode find-or-creates its type by name (reuse keeps episodes grouped).
- **Endpoint `POST /api/extract/[sessionId]/commit`.** Auth-derived patient/user; rejects failed/already-committed sessions (409). Commits the sent cards sequentially (deterministic shared side effects, e.g. a symptom type born from card 1 reused by card 2). `finalize` (client sets it when the batch clears the last pending card) → `extractionSessionQueries.markCommitted` flips session + report → `committed` in one txn. **E5 seam:** fire-and-forget debounced insight-gen fires here once E5 lands.
- **Surface `components/extract/extraction-confirmation.tsx`.** Split-panel (source ~40% sticky / cards ~60% scroll); group-by-type headers; ambiguity inline-resolve chips (block Confirm until answered; `intent` ambiguity answered by the toggle; target field renders `(pending)`); new-vs-update toggle w/ preview text + card-state pill (`confident`/`needs your call`); per-field inline `edit`; per-card Confirm/Discard/`Edit manually instead`; `Confirm all · N` greying to `· N blocked`; failure state (dashed + "things that usually help" + `replace file →`). NO floating Ask AI (§6.11:1729). Reads untyped jsonb via `parseStoredExtractionOutput` (never cast).
- **"Edit manually instead" prefill.** `lib/extract/draft.ts` — single-use sessionStorage draft (namespaced by type, keeps PHI out of the URL). Card writes the draft + navigates to `…/[segment]/new` + discards itself. **Consumer wired for medication only** (reference vertical, canonical prescription path) via `takeExtractionDraft` in a state initializer (StrictMode-safe) merged over form defaults. The other 7 /new forms adopt the same 3-line hook as a fast-follow — write-side + mechanism are in place.
**Deviations (surfaced):**
- **`Confirm all` navigates to the patient profile, not the dashboard, and shows no toast** (§6.11:1807) — E0a dashboard isn't built and no toast lib is installed. Revisit when E0a lands.
- **UPDATE commit is state-entities-only by design** (event types always create, §5.4:791) — not a gap.
- **Prefill consumed by the medication form only this pass** — remaining 7 forms pending.
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` clean across all new/changed files (commit engine, endpoint, surface, page, draft util, symptom/extraction-session queries, medication form). Browser `verify-ui` pass of the live extract→confirm→commit loop pending (needs a seeded `ready_for_confirmation` session).

**Date:** 2026-07-07
**Decision:** **Logging from full-screen chat now lives in the conversation (§6.2:1197) — reverses the E1 "a log is not a chat turn, doesn't persist" call.** Fixes three issues found in live testing: (1) the typed log wasn't echoed (only a "Reading that…" spinner during the multi-second extraction), (2) commit returned to the patient profile instead of the chat, (3) the chat session didn't persist — it vanished from the CHATS list.
**Context:** E1 (2026-06-30) deliberately made the `log` branch stateless: no session, no persisted turn, navigate straight to the confirmation screen. User feedback during E3 testing: logging from chat should feel like part of the conversation, and confirming should return to where they came from. This is the §6.2:1197 refinement that was explicitly deferred; the user pulled it forward.
**Choice:**
- **`runLog` now `ensureSession()`s first** (creating/persisting the chat session, same lazy path as questions), **optimistically echoes** the user's message via `useChat`'s `setMessages` (so it's visible immediately during extraction), then navigates to the confirmation screen with `?returnTo=/patient/[id]/chat/[sid]`.
- **Server persists the log as a real user turn.** `/api/chat/quick-log` accepts an optional `sessionId`; when present it scope-checks the session, `addMessage(user, text)`, and `setTitleIfNull(titleFromText(text))` BEFORE running the (several-second) extraction — so the turn survives the trip and the session is findable/titled. A foreign/stale id is skipped, not fatal. `titleFromText` exported from `lib/chat/quick-log.ts` and reused.
- **Return-to-origin.** The confirmation page reads `returnTo` from the query string, accepts only a same-origin absolute path (`/^\/[^/]/` — no `//` or scheme, guards open-redirect), and both `Confirm all` (on finalize) and `Discard all` route back there. Upload path has no origin → still lands on the patient profile (no dashboard yet, E0a).
- **Optimistic echo reconciles, not duplicates:** the local `local-<ts>` message is replaced by the DB copy when the session reopens (server-loaded `initialMessages`).
**Deferred (still open from §6.2:1197):** the post-commit assistant **"Logged ✓ N item(s)"** confirmation message in the chat — returning shows the user's log turn but no AI acknowledgement yet. Needs the commit endpoint (or a follow-up call) to write an assistant turn to the session; queued.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. Live browser smoke (`verify-log-flow.mjs`, one real extraction, Discard-all so no vault write, tracked cleanup): message echoed during routing ✅, `returnTo`→chat ✅, session persists with the log message + appears in CHATS list ✅, Discard all returns to the chat session ✅, console clean.
