**Date:** 2026-07-08
**Decision:** **Wired "Edit manually instead" prefill into the remaining 7 /new forms (condition, doctor, allergy, vital, visit, symptom, lab) — all 8 now consume the extraction draft. Closes the deferred fast-follow.**
**Context:** E3 shipped prefill for the medication form only; the other 7 opened blank. User asked to finish it (after the note surfaced twice). Each form needed its own snake_case→form-shape mapper (different fields/enums; UUID picker fields can't take a free-text name).
**Choice:**
- **Shared coercion helpers** added to `lib/extract/draft.ts` (`draftString` / `draftEnum` / `draftDate`) so each mapper stays ~10-15 lines; enum fields drop unless they match a known value (never guessed), mirroring the commit mapper.
- **Per-form mappers** (`draftToXValues`) merged over each form's `defaultValues`. Notable handling: **vital** splits a BP `value` ("152/95" → primary/secondary) and defaults unit by reading type; **lab** maps the `results[]` array into marker rows and splits `reference_range` ("70-100"/"<100"/">5") into low/high; **symptom** sets `typeSelection = NEW_TYPE` + `newTypeName` from the extracted name; **visit/lab** leave the doctor/orderedBy **UUID picker fields blank** (the agent gives a free-text name — the user selects), per "prefill plain fields, leave entity-links blank." measured_at/started_at timestamps aren't prefilled (datetime-local tz conversion) — forms keep "now."
- **StrictMode-safe single-use read.** `takeExtractionDraft` clears sessionStorage on read, which is unsafe in a `useState` initializer — React StrictMode double-invokes initializers in dev, so the first call consumes the draft and the second gets null. Fixed with a short-lived per-type module memo: the paired synchronous invocation reuses the cached value, then a `setTimeout(0)` drops it so a *later* "+ Add" navigation doesn't re-serve a consumed draft. `writeExtractionDraft` also clears the memo eagerly.
- **Symptom guard:** the draft is only read in the open "Log symptom" flow (`lockedType == null`) — a locked entry from a symptom's page keeps its type and must not consume the draft.
**Non-issue clarified (was a red herring during verify):** a `page.goto('/…/new')` full-page load with a draft in sessionStorage hydration-mismatches (server renders blank, client prefilled) and React resets inputs. This **never happens in the real flow** — "Edit manually instead" uses `router.push` (client-side nav, no SSR hydration), and the draft is consumed on that first mount, so a later full reload finds sessionStorage already cleared. No code change needed; the defaultValues-merge approach is correct for the real path.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. Deterministic browser run (`verify-editmanual.mjs`, seeded 7-card session, drove the real "Edit manually instead" client-nav per card): **all 7 forms prefill correctly** — condition/doctor/allergy/vital(BP split)/visit/symptom/lab(marker row + ref-range split) — console clean, no hydration errors. Test rows cleaned (report delete cascades the session).

**Date:** 2026-07-08
**Decision:** **Backfilled the Delete action into the medication detail `…` menu — medication was the only entity missing it, contrary to §6.5:1388 ("Discontinue, Resolve, Delete, Export").** Surfaced during E3 testing: user in the medication detail page saw only Discontinue.
**Context:** Medication was the Phase C reference impl, deliberately scoped to Discontinue-only (menu comment: "Item 4 scope: Discontinue only. Item 5 adds Edit / Delete / Export"). Every Phase-D entity cloned that menu and added Delete, but medication was never back-filled. The `DELETE /api/medications/[id]` route + `medicationQueries.delete` already existed — only the UI entry point was missing.
**Choice:**
- **New `medication-delete-dialog.tsx`** clones the established condition/allergy delete-dialog pattern: destructive confirm → `DELETE` → redirect to the medications list. Copy notes it removes the change history and points to Discontinue as the keep-history path.
- **Menu now renders Delete (always, destructive-styled) + Discontinue (only when status ≠ `discontinued`).** Discontinue is a logged state change that only applies to an active med; Delete removes the record outright and stays available in every state.
- **Detail page always renders the menu.** Previously it suppressed the entire menu for discontinued meds (`showActionsMenu = status !== "discontinued"`) since the sole item was Discontinue — which would have left a discontinued med undeletable. Removed that gate; passes `patientId` + `status` through.
- **Cascade scope:** delete removes the med and its `medication_changes` (onDelete:"cascade"); insights cite it polymorphically via jsonb (not an FK) so a stale citation simply won't resolve — matches condition/lab delete behavior. Export deferred (as it was originally).
**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean. Browser `verify-ui` pass: active med shows Discontinue + Delete; discontinued med shows Delete and hides Discontinue (menu now present at all); Delete → confirm copy → redirect to list; deleted row 404s with change-log cascaded; Cancel leaves the record intact; console clean. Throwaway rows torn down.

**Date:** 2026-07-10
**Decision:** **Two E3 log-flow fixes from user testing: (1) discontinuing a medication via a chat log now works; (2) a "Logged ✓" acknowledgement is written back to the chat after a commit.**
**Context:** User logged "He's not taking Amlodipine anymore" → confirmed → the med wasn't discontinued. And after any log-commit, returning to the chat showed only the user's own message, with no sign anything happened.
**Choice:**
- **(1) Discontinuation.** Root cause: the extraction agent's medication fields had NO status concept, so "not taking anymore" produced either a nulled field (nothing to commit) or a stray notes edit — and `updateMedication` only handled dose/frequency/notes, never status. Fixed both ends: (a) added a `status` field ("active"|"paused"|"discontinued") to the medication extraction prompt with guidance ("no longer taking" → update with status discontinued); (b) `updateMedication` now routes status "discontinued" to `medicationQueries.discontinue` (terminal — appends the status change + stamps discontinuedOn, then returns) and status "paused" to the change-log. `CommitContext` gained `timezone` (discontinue stamps discontinuedOn in patient-local day). Verified: extraction emits `status:"discontinued"`; commit flips a throwaway med to discontinued with discontinuedOn set.
- **(2) Logged-ack (§6.2:1197).** The chat session id now rides the log→confirmation→commit chain (`?chatSessionId=` on the extract URL from both the full-screen and drawer runLog; `commitRequestSchema.chatSessionId`). On finalize, the commit endpoint scope-checks the session and writes an assistant turn — `summarizeCommit(cards, results)` builds a per-card bulleted summary ("Done — I've logged that to the record: • Discontinued Amlodipine"). Brand voice, no model call. Verified in-browser: the ack shows on return. **Accepted limitation:** per-card confirms (vs "Confirm all") only summarize the finalize request's card, not the whole session — noted.
- **Hook fix (unrelated):** the PreToolUse Bash safety hook used an unsupported `if` field (so it ran on every Bash call) and grepped `-f\b` (matching any `-f` flag, e.g. `pkill -f`), spuriously blocking with "Force-push blocked." Rewritten to only trigger on `git push` + an actual force flag. Takes effect next session (hooks load at startup).
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean across all changed files; deterministic browser checks for both the discontinue commit and the logged-ack. Uncommitted.

**Date:** 2026-07-10
**Decision:** **Quick-log placeholder Reports no longer surface as user-facing Reports — excluded from the §6.6 timeline and the synthesis vault context.**
**Context:** User logged "He stopped taking Glimepiride" via chat and saw it appear as a Report in the Reports list. Validated: `processQuickLog` creates a Report holding the typed text (the extraction source + `source_report_id` target, §5.4), and `reportQueries.forPatient` (backing the timeline + vault context) returned ALL reports unfiltered — 18 of 20 reports in the demo vault were quick-log text stubs (type=null, no file), only 2 real. A typed note isn't a document; it was cluttering both the timeline and the AI's reasoning context.
**Choice:**
- **Structural discriminator (no schema change):** a quick-log stub = *has an extraction_session AND no `source_file_url`*. An uploaded document has a file; a manually-entered report has no extraction_session — both stay visible. (The §5.4 design intended a `source_text` field on entities; the schema only has `source_report_id`, so the placeholder-Report approach stands — we just hide the stubs from user surfaces.)
- **`reportQueries.forTimeline(patientId)`** — `forPatient` filtered by `sourceFileUrl IS NOT NULL OR NOT EXISTS(extraction_session for this report)`. The §6.6 reports page and `buildVaultContext` now use it. `forPatient` is unchanged (still used by `bySlug`/internal); stubs remain fetchable by id, so an entity's `source_report_id` link still resolves to its note — it's just off the list.
- **Existing stubs left in place** (hidden, not deleted) — deleting the source-linked ones would null a real entity's `source_report_id`. A cleanup of the truly-orphan stubs (never-confirmed `extracting` rows, discontinue/update stubs with no referrer) is offered as optional follow-up.
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean; `forTimeline` returns 2/20 (the real endo note + cardiology prescription); browser check confirms the timeline shows the real reports and none of the quick-log notes.

**Follow-up (same day):** ran the orphan-stub cleanup — `scripts/cleanup-orphan-source-stubs.ts` (deletes only stubs no entity references via source_report_id; deleting a Report cascades its extraction_session). Result on the demo vault: 18 stubs → 17 orphans deleted, 1 kept (source-linked). Reports now: 3 total (2 real + 1 kept stub), timeline shows 2. Script kept for repeat maintenance.

**Follow-up (rail count):** the wiki-rail Reports badge counted ALL reports (plain `count()`, incl. stubs) while the timeline used `forTimeline` — so the badge showed 4 vs the list's 2. Extracted the stub-excluding predicate to a shared `realReportsWhere(patientId)` (db/queries/report.ts), now used by both `forTimeline` and `wikiCounts` so the count and the list can't drift. Verified: badge 2 = timeline 2.

**Date:** 2026-07-14
**Decision:** **Give the extraction agent today's date + a lean, bounded view of recent event records (labs/visits), so it stops guessing years and can reason about existing entities. Kept extraction single-shot (no agentic DB-search) — deliberate.**
**Context:** User logged "add to the June 15th lab that his creatinine was 1.7" and the confirmation asked "June 15 2025 or 2024?" — never suggesting 2026, blind to the single existing 2026-06-15 lab. Root cause: `buildMatchingDictionary` fed the agent ONLY state entities (meds/conditions/doctors/allergies) and NO current date; its guardrail said "never guess a date", so with no anchor it flagged a year ambiguity and fell back to a training-data-past prior. Discussed the alternative of giving the agent DB-search tools (agentic retrieval); concluded that's the right v2 direction but at single-patient scale it's slower, pricier (multi-hop loops re-send context), and less deterministic than a lean single-shot dictionary — and it's the design's already-deferred "no RAG in v1" roadmap. Chose the cheap deterministic middle.
**Choice:**
- **Today's date injected** — `runExtraction({..., today})` (callers pass `todayInTimezone(tz)`); `buildMatchingDictionary` header states the date and instructs resolving relative/partial dates against it. The "never guess a date" guardrail softened to "don't invent a date the source doesn't imply, but DO resolve relative/partial dates against today."
- **Lean bounded event context** — the dictionary now also lists recent lab reports (name+date+id) and visits (date+doctor+id), most-recent 12 each. Explicitly labelled **context-only**: events stay always-create-new (§5.4), NOT match targets — the agent is told not to set `matched_entity_id` on them, and to flag (raise an ambiguity) when a note is clearly amending an existing event, since amendment lives on the entity's own screen. (Commit is defensive too: an `update` on a non-state type is already coerced to create.) Bounded/lean per the cost reasoning — not the full vault.
- **NOT done (deferred, scoped separately):** actual lab/visit amendment via extraction+commit (needs the §6.7 correction path wired into commit) and agentic DB-search retrieval (v1.5/v2, and best applied to synthesis + large vaults first).
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. Live probe on the exact input now returns `report_date: "2026-06-15"` (year resolved), names the existing Apollo June-15 lab, and raises an honest "I can only create new here — amend from the report's screen" ambiguity instead of the "2025 or 2024?" nonsense.

**Date:** 2026-07-14
**Decision:** **High-effort `/code-review` (workflow-backed) run on the session's E3 + chat-log + stub-filtering + extraction-context work — 6 CONFIRMED must-fix regressions queued for the next session (fix before building further).**
**Context:** Reviewed commits `d263b10`+`338ae67` + the uncommitted `summarizeCommit` tweak. Most serious findings are self-inflicted by this session's two big changes (report-stub filtering + logging-lives-in-chat).
**The 6 must-fix (detail + locations in progress.md Next Steps):**
- `vault-context.ts:158` — `forTimeline` in synthesis context breaks `source_report_id` citations for every chat-committed entity → `§ unresolved:<uuid>` (violates citation discipline). Fix: exclude stubs from timeline/rail but KEEP them in the citation slug-index.
- `commit.ts:448` — dose/freq update on a discontinued med throws `MedicationDomainError` (not `CommitBlocked`) → 500 aborts the sequential batch mid-way → duplicate writes on retry. Fix: `commitCard` converts any domain error → CommitBlocked.
- `extraction-confirmation.tsx:205` — discarding the last card strands the user (no finalize, header controls gone).
- `quick-log/route.ts:45` + `chat-conversation.tsx:277` — failed/discarded logs leave a dangling turn + titled empty session + un-cleared echo with no error signal.
- `report.ts:123` — entity-less stubs vanish from all surfaces.
- `medication-form.tsx:86` — `MED_CATEGORIES` missing `OTC`, `MED_FORMS` missing `other` (real enum values) → prefill drops them.
**Lower (PLAUSIBLE/cleanup):** uncertain-intent event card un-committable; date guardrail ignores document age; per-card full-table fetches in `createVisit`/`createSymptomEpisode`.
**Note:** these are net-new regressions from good features — the features stay, the cleanup pass is the next session's opener. Roots trace to two intentional design moves (stubs off user surfaces; logs persist as chat turns) whose edge cases weren't fully handled.

---

**Date:** 2026-07-14
**Decision:** **Landed all 6 CONFIRMED code-review regressions from the prior entry.** tsc + eslint clean; DB-level fixes (1/2/5) verified by a throwaway probe against arogya-dev (tracked cleanup).
**Context:** The self-inflicted regressions from this session's stub-hiding + logs-live-in-chat changes. Notes below record the non-obvious judgment calls; the finding list itself is the prior entry.
**Choice:**
- **(1) Citation resolution (`vault-context.ts`).** Synthesis now does TWO report reads: `forPatient` (all, incl. quick-log stubs) feeds ONLY the citation slug-index; `forTimeline` (real docs) feeds the serialized Reports section. So a chat-committed entity's `source_report_id` resolves to `§ report:<date>` instead of `§ unresolved:<uuid>`, while stubs stay out of the AI's reading context. Tradeoff: a stub sharing a real report's date can now take a `-2` collision suffix — accepted (unresolved citations are worse).
- **(2) Domain error → per-card block (`commit.ts`).** `commitCard` now converts any `*DomainError` (detected by `name.endsWith("DomainError")`) into `{ok:false, error}` — a dose/freq update on an already-discontinued med (`MedicationDomainError`) no longer 500s the route and aborts the sequential batch (which duplicated earlier writes on retry). Mapped copy for `medication_discontinued`/`already_discontinued`; generic voice-compliant fallback otherwise.
- **(3) Last-card discard strands the user (`extraction-confirmation.tsx`).** Split `discard` into a pure `markDiscarded` (used by "Edit manually") + a handler that calls `endReview` when the pending set empties. `endReview` finalizes the session (a **finalize-only commit** — `{cards:[], finalize:true}`, needing `commitRequestSchema.cards` relaxed from `.min(1)`) when anything was committed, so the stub report flips to `committed` (else it stays `extracting` and re-breaks fix 1). `discardAll` routed through the same path.
- **(4) Failed/discarded log cleanup (`quick-log/route.ts` + `chat-conversation.tsx` + extract page).** Server persists the user turn + title only AFTER extraction succeeds (a failed extraction no longer leaves a dangling turn + titled empty session). Client adds a `logError` state (inline error + "Try again", keeping the echoed turn). A session created solely for this log carries `newSession=1` to the confirmation page, which deletes it on a full discard (no dangling turn / titled empty session) and lands on the chat index.
- **(5) Entity-less stubs no longer vanish (`report.ts`).** `realReportsWhere` hides a quick-log stub ONLY once it has produced a source-linked entity (redundant with it). An entity-LESS stub (failed extraction / full discard) stays visible — hiding it would silently lose the user's typed note, which for a health record is worse than a stray timeline card. Adds a 7-table `hasNoSourceLinkedEntity()` NOT-EXISTS branch.
- **(6) Enum prefill drop (`medication-form.tsx`).** `MED_FORM_VALUES`/`MED_CATEGORY_VALUES` now derive from the shared `FORM_OPTIONS`/`CATEGORY_OPTIONS` (typed against the real enums) instead of a stale hardcoded list that dropped `other`/`OTC`; matched case-insensitively to the canonical value (`otc` → `OTC`).
**Alternatives considered:** (5) delete abandoned stubs at their lifecycle points instead of keeping them visible — rejected as heavier (needs endpoints; failed-extraction stub is still needed to render the §6.11 failure state) and riskier than a visibility rule; the manual `cleanup-orphan-source-stubs.ts` still exists for true cruft. (3) a dedicated "close session" endpoint vs. reusing commit with empty cards — reused commit (route already guards session state).
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean across all 9 changed files; existing `vault-context:check` still green (determinism intact); probe asserted fix 5 (entity-less stub on timeline, entity-producing stub hidden), fix 1 (med's source citation resolves, stub note not serialized), fix 2 (dose-on-discontinued returns ok:false, no throw, voice-compliant message).

**Date:** 2026-07-14
**Decision:** **Entity amendment through the extraction agent — full create+update parity for lab / visit / symptom (vitals stay immutable); deletions declined in-chat; readable lab card with `old → new`.** Closes the deferred "lab/visit amendment" item.
**Context:** The north-star sentence ("add creatinine 1.7 to the June 15 lab") is an *amendment* of an existing event, but events were create-only through the agent (`STATE_TYPES` gated `update`; extraction told never to set `matched_entity_id` on events). User asked for parity: whatever medication supports through the agent, other entities should too. Clarified that medication's agent ops are create+update (NOT delete — delete is UI-only), and that the §5.4 "always create-new" rule is about not conflating separate draws, not a ban on amending one record (§6.7 already has `+ Log a correction`).
**Choice:**
- **Amendable events = lab_report, visit, symptom_episode.** `commit.ts`: `AMENDABLE_EVENT_TYPES` ∪ `STATE_TYPES` = `UPDATABLE_TYPES`; new `updateLabReport` (append missing markers + **correct** existing ones in place via `correctResult`; `labResultQueries.addResults` for appends), `updateVisit` (correct date/doctor/reason/summary, append notes), `updateSymptomEpisode` (correct severity, append notes). Overwrite-in-place because events have no change log (§6.7 / §4:513).
- **Vitals immutable (user call).** No amendment path — §4:433 (delete + re-enter). Extraction prompt + dictionary say so explicitly.
- **Deletions declined as a chat reply (not a confirmation detour).** New optional `notice` on the extraction output schema. A delete request → 0 extractions + `notice`; `processQuickLog` returns `{kind:"declined", notice}` and creates **no** stub Report; the quick-log route writes the advisory as an assistant turn and the chat shows it inline. State changes ("stopped taking X" → discontinued) are still updates, not deletions.
- **Legible confirmation cards.** Lab update card renders a Markers view (names the target report; `1.9 → 1.5 · correcting`; `4.2 · new marker`) instead of a JSON blob; visit/symptom overwrite fields show `old → new` (page passes `labSnapshots` / `fieldSnapshots`; notes append, so excluded from the diff).
- **Matching dictionary** now lists recent symptom episodes (bounded 12) alongside labs/visits; event block instructs correct-or-amend across the three.
- **§5.4 doc-fix** landed inline (amendment carve-out block) so code and spec agree.
**/check fixes applied before commit (spec-compliance review, this session):** (#1, blocker) `updateLabReport` no longer nulls an existing marker value when a matched marker carries no value — value-less markers are skipped, never written; (#2) it blocks a true no-op like every sibling update path (tracks a corrected/appended count); (#4) visit/symptom note-append dedup uses exact-match (`!== current.notes`) not `.includes()` (which false-skipped a substring addition); (#3) `old → new` extended to visit summary + symptom severity (the overwrite paths — the safety rationale that justified the lab diff); (#5) decline no longer leaves an entity-less stub on the timeline (chat-inline, above); (#6) `processUpload` notes why it has no notice branch (documents can't ask to delete); (#7) re-indented the field-list map.
**Alternatives considered:** delete-through-the-agent (rejected — most "removals" are status changes that preserve history; hard-delete from a fuzzy chat log is a footgun, keep it a deliberate UI action); lab amendment append-only (rejected after user review — in-flow correction is safe given the human-in-the-loop card + `old → new`, and the on-page dialog already overwrites in place with no history); decline via a confirmation-page NoticeState (built then replaced — a refusal is a conversational reply, and the stub it left surfaced as a timeline "report").
**Verified:** `tsc` + `eslint` clean. DB probes: lab correct/append + append-only-value-preservation, value-less-skip, no-op block, visit/symptom update, decline-creates-no-stub. Live extraction: delete → `notice`; "was actually 1.5" → `update` with corrected value. Browser: readable lab card (correcting/new-marker/target-report), visit+symptom `old → new` (drops in create mode), decline shows inline in chat with no navigation. All ZZZ rows swept. **Uncommitted — pending user review.**

**Follow-up (2nd /check, same session):** two refinements applied. (A) `updateLabReport` correction now PRESERVES an unrestated value (`value: r.value ?? match.value`) instead of gating the whole iteration on `hasValue` — removes the value-null risk *by construction* and lets a value-less unit/flag/range correction apply (was silently skipped); the `changed`-count guard still blocks true no-ops, and the append branch still requires a value. (D) the in-chat decline now calls `onPersisted()` so a fresh session's server-set title reaches the CHATS list (that path doesn't navigate/stream). Probe-verified: value-less correction blocked + value preserved, flag-only correction applies with value intact, real correction + append guards hold. No blockers; 2nd pass otherwise clean.

**Date:** 2026-07-17
**Decision:** **Shift the chat-logging agent from pure faithful-transcriber toward a "guided scribe" — surface + help capture high-value fields the user didn't state, without ever fabricating them.** Three-step plan; step 1 (modal discoverability) building now.
**Context:** User manually logged "he was diagnosed with osteoporosis" → the confirmation card showed only name + status (all extraction stated). Correct per §5.4 never-fabricate, but a chat health-logger should intelligently prompt for useful missing info (severity, category, diagnosis date, diagnosed-by, managing doctor), not just transcribe the bare input. Never-fabricate stays sacred; eliciting ≠ inventing.
**Choice:**
- **Boundary:** split missing fields into (a) *general facts about the condition* (taxonomy/category — safely inferable, a lookup not a diagnosis) vs (b) *patient-specific facts* (severity, dates, which doctor — must be ASKED, never inferred). Agent-side inference of (a) is a separate future prompt decision (borderline vs "no medical interpretation") — NOT bundled here.
- **Step 1 — modal discoverability (building):** the confirmation card surfaces the entity's high-value *empty* fields as optional inline chips (text / enum-select / date), so the user can add them without the form detour. Scoped to fields the commit create-mapper persists; enum values sourced from the real schema (no stale-list drift, cf. the 2026-07-14 medication-form bug). `createCondition` gains `diagnosed_on`. **Doctor-link FK fields deferred** (diagnosed_by / managing_doctor / prescribing_doctor / ordering_doctor) — need the standing doctor-picker carryover.
- **Step 2 — a single bounded chat nudge (next):** before the confirmation opens, ONE optional "want to add X?" turn, only when the entity is worth enriching (new chronic condition yes; dose tweak no), always skippable. Stateful; keep to one round.
- **Step 3 — full per-log interview: HELD** — overlaps E4 onboarding (8-phase interviewer) + friction risk; prototype after E5.
**Reasoning:** a richer, better-sourced vault directly serves the §1.3 north-star ("diagnosed by Dr. Sharma, April 3rd"). Discoverability-first is small/safe and fixes the "didn't know I could add that" gap; the chat nudge adds the thinking-partner feel without turning every log into an interrogation. Must not derail E5.

**Follow-up (step 2 built, same session 2026-07-17):** the enrichment nudge is agent-decided, not hardcoded — the extraction agent emits an optional top-level `enrichment.question` (guided by a short prompt section: ask when a record is sparse and useful context is missing, omit when complete/trivial/a targeted update; one question, always optional, no cap on how much it asks). Zero added cost for the decision (folded into the existing extraction pass); only *answering* costs a second extraction. Flow: `processQuickLog` returns `{kind:"nudge", question, extractionSessionId}` (session persisted so skip is a free navigation); the chat shows the question + "Just log it", the next input is the answer → re-extracted as original-log + answer into the SAME session (`reuseSessionId`, no orphan) → enriched confirmation. Note: the nudge fires independent of extraction count — a too-vague log ("a blood pressure pill", no name) yields 0 extractions + a question rather than the failure state. Verified: live probe (sparse condition + vague med nudge; complete BP reading doesn't; reuse enriches Glaucoma→severity) + browser (answer & skip paths). Step 3 (full per-log interview) still HELD.

**Date:** 2026-07-19
**Decision:** **Agent classifies high-confidence taxonomy fields from a stated entity (condition `category`, symptom `body_area`, medication/allergy `category`) — resolving the (a)-side inference deferred on 2026-07-17. Plus a `.nullish()` output-schema crash fix and three guided-scribe (step 2) hardening fixes. Shipped as two commits (A classification, B guided-scribe).**
**Context:** User: a logged "wrist pain" symptom showed no Body Area, and conditions showed no Category — fields trivially inferable from the stated entity. The 2026-07-17 entry split missing fields into (a) *general facts about the entity* (taxonomy — safely inferable) vs (b) *patient-specific facts* (must be asked), and explicitly deferred (a) as "borderline vs no-medical-interpretation." This resolves (a): filing a stated diagnosis/symptom into its schema enum is STRUCTURING (the same act as "hypertension"→Condition), not interpreting clinical significance. §4:301 calls condition category "for dashboard grouping and AI reasoning scope"; §4:446 defines `body_area`; §5.4:799's "no interpretation" is about not editorializing on significance, not a ban on taxonomy.
**Choice:**
- Extraction prompt gains a "Classifying into arogya's taxonomy" section drawing the file-vs-invent line: `condition.category`, `symptom_episode.body_area`, `medication.category`, `allergy.category` are HIGH-CONFIDENCE classifications of an already-stated entity — set them with no ambiguity/enrichment question. Fabricating a clinical VALUE never given (dose/date/range) stays forbidden.
- Prompt enum lists are mirrored BY HAND from the pgEnums (a comment names the source enums); drift degrades safely via `commit.ts` `enumMember` (drops unknown values rather than writing them).
- `commit.ts` `createSymptomEpisode` threads `body_area` into NEW symptom-type creation (existing types keep their area — no backfill). `createCondition` already read `data.category`. `enrichment.ts` adds enum-select chips for medication category + symptom body_area (condition/allergy category were already present).
- **Output-schema fix:** `notice`/`enrichment` → `.nullish()` (was `.optional()`). The model sometimes emits explicit `null` on the empty "couldn't read it" path, which `.optional()` rejected → `parse_failure` crash instead of the §6.11 failure state.
- **Guided-scribe step 2 hardening (from /check):** (1) a zero-entity nudge now threads `hasEntities` through `processQuickLog`→route→chat, so "skip" on a nothing-extracted nudge DISMISSES in-chat ("Never mind") instead of navigating to the empty→failure confirmation; (2) a failed nudge-answer POST keeps the nudge open so retry re-extracts via reuse (not re-routed through the classifier as a fresh log) — `answerNudge` gains an `alreadyEchoed` param + a nudge-aware "Try again".
**Alternatives considered:** agent-side inference of (a) was held as too-borderline on 2026-07-17 — reversed here now that the file-vs-invent boundary is crisp and enum-guarded. For the zero-entity nudge: a distinct empty-but-not-failed state on the confirmation page (broader blast radius — the §6.11 `extractions.length===0`→failure branch is relied on for the genuine unreadable case) — rejected in favor of the localized `hasEntities` thread.
**Reasoning:** a denser, well-structured vault at capture time directly serves the §1.3 north-star (symptom clustering needs `body_area`; category feeds the "AI reasoning scope" the doc names). Classification is upstream of every synthesis.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean across all touched files. `extraction:check` (new cases): wrist pain → `body_area=arms`; "diagnosed with hypothyroidism" → new condition `category=endocrine`; gibberish → clean empty array (no parse crash). `quick-log:check` (new nudge case): sparse "new blood pressure pill" → nudge `hasEntities=false`; scenario 1 relaxed to accept confirm-or-nudge (guided-scribe legitimately nudges a loggable note). **Committed in two batches.**

**Date:** 2026-07-19
**Decision:** **External `↗` citations stay inert (non-clickable) through the end of Phase E; making them clickable is deferred to Phase F as a deliberate architectural choice — leaning toward web-search-grounded URLs.**
**Context:** Manual E-testing surfaced that a synthesis "grounded in" response listed external guideline refs (`↗ KDIGO 2022 diabetes-in-CKD`, `↗ NICE NG136`) that aren't clickable. This is a KNOWN deviation from the design doc — §5.3:737 ("every external claim attributed with a clickable URL"), :646 (external citations as a links list), :694 (References block). Current impl: the inline `↗ source-name` syntax carries only a NAME, not a URL (`parse.ts` external segment), and `CitationPill` renders it as an inert neutral span BY DESIGN (`citation-pill.tsx:15-18`) — nothing to link to.
**Choice:** Keep inert for now (honest as-is — a neutral "look this up" marker, not a broken promise); revisit after Phase E. Three options weighed:
- (A) **Web-search grounded** — synthesis uses a real web-search tool so cited URLs are ones it actually retrieved; real by construction. Costs: per-chat latency + tokens; crosses Phase 5's "no RAG in v1"; must gate so search fires only when the answer leans external, not for vault-answerable questions. **Leaning this way.**
- (B) Curated source allowlist — agent cites only from a small hand-verified URL set; brittle, limited coverage, ongoing maintenance; clickable for a few sources and inert for the rest (inconsistent).
- (C) Prompt-emitted URLs from model memory — fastest, but the hallucination path (dead/wrong/superseded links). Rejected for a product whose thesis is citation trust: a confidently-wrong link erodes trust in EVERY citation, including the reliable vault `§` pills.
**Alternatives considered:** shipping (C) now for demo polish — rejected (a dead link in a health-app portfolio demo is a credibility own-goal); pre-validating emitted URLs with a server HEAD request — rejected (latency per link, and a 200 still doesn't prove it's the right/current page).
**Reasoning:** Not on the north-star path — the §1.3 demo moment is pure vault synthesis with `§` pills, zero external URLs; E5 (insight generator) is higher-leverage. But when built it must be REAL (A), not a guess (C) or a half-measure (B). The insight schema already models real external refs (`external_refs {title,url,snippet}`, §4:581), so chat synthesis would follow that shape. Closing this needs a doc-fix either way (code adopts §5.3:737, or the doc records the v1 deferral). Verify Anthropic's current web-search tool against the docs before building.

**Date:** 2026-07-20
**Decision:** **The post-commit chat acknowledgement is now a SYNTHESIS response (engages with what was logged + cites the record), not a mechanical "Done — I've logged" receipt. Closes the "an utterance can be both a log AND a conversation" gap.**
**Context:** During manual E-testing: dropping a new fact mid-conversation ("he also had headaches these last couple of weeks") got hard-routed to logging, and the flow dead-ended at a mechanical receipt instead of continuing the conversation. Root cause: the router hard-splits every input into one of {question, log, ambiguous}, and the log path terminated in `summarizeCommit`'s fixed string with no synthesis. Yet the router prompt already PROMISED "the system handles the question automatically after the log is confirmed" (§5.5), and §6.2:1203 says logging from chat "returns inline confirmation … and the conversation continues" — the second half was never implemented.
**Choice:**
- At commit-finalize (`app/api/extract/[sessionId]/commit/route.ts`), the assistant turn written back to the chat is now generated by the synthesis agent via a new `buildLogAck` helper, instead of `summarizeCommit`. It runs AFTER the entities are committed, so they're already in the vault and citable.
- New runtime `LOG_ACK_ADDENDUM` mode on synthesis (the `PATIENT_NAMING_ADDENDUM` pattern — the verbatim §10.3 prompt is untouched): acknowledge in 1–2 natural sentences and engage with `§` citations ONLY when there's real signal; a brief warm ack otherwise; no manufactured insight, no celebratory language, no thanking. `runSynthesis` gains an optional `systemAddendum` param.
- `committedForAck` (in `commit.ts`, reuses `phraseForCard`) is the factual anchor passed as a system-note user message; synthesis reads full entity detail from the vault.
- **Fail-safe:** any synthesis error falls back to the deterministic `summarizeCommit` string, so the ack never fails to appear. Empty/finalize-only commits produce no ack (unchanged). Normal-chat `composedSystem` is byte-identical when no addendum is passed → Opus prompt-cache + verbatim-prompt determinism intact.
**Alternatives considered:** (1) let a single agent freely "decide log vs. discuss and act" — rejected: erodes the human-in-the-loop confirmation tripwire (extraction never auto-writes) and hurts predictability/testability. Kept the lightweight classifier + confirmation gate; only the post-commit turn changed. (2) generate the ack BEFORE commit (respond to the unsaved fact) — rejected: it couldn't cite the entity as a vault fact without a cite-before-save mismatch if the user then discards; post-commit is the correct citing point. (3) a distinct empty-but-not-failed confirmation state — not needed here.
**Deviation flagged:** §6.2:1203's *example* is a terse "Logged: BP 152/95 — see [BP page →]"; this goes richer (a full cited synthesis turn). It fulfills the section's stated intent ("the conversation continues") + the §5.5 contract, and the "see [page →]" affordance is satisfied by clickable `§` pills. Deliberate enhancement, not silent drift.
**Known cost / follow-up:** the Opus call blocks the commit-finalize response ~4–6s, absorbed by the per-card "committing" state. The proper fix is the deferred §6.2:1197 inline-confirm (no navigation + a STREAMED ack in the conversation) — this build composes cleanly into that. No durable regression test (the ack needs a full session); covered by a live probe + `verify-ui`.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean. Live probe (3 logs): BP 152/95 → cited connection to the telmisartan history + a Dr. Menon flag; headache → connected to dizziness/BP; dust allergy → brief ack, no manufactured insight (restraint held). `verify-ui`: the chat shows an 8-pill synthesis ack, not the receipt. Full agent run-through passed (router 6/6, extraction 8/8, synthesis 4/4). **Committed 2026-07-20.**

**Date:** 2026-07-20
**Decision:** **E5 insight generator shipped (§5.6 / §9.3 / §10.3) — Opus agent + `insight_runs` debounce table (schema addition SIGNED OFF) + fire-and-forget orchestrator + 13 server-side triggers + seed insights retired. Zero surface change; the §6.8/6.9 feed now shows only real generator output.**
**Context:** The last piece of the extract→confirm→commit→insight loop and the §1.3 north-star generator. Everything downstream existed (insights table + surfaces from Phase D, locked Zod output schema, `includeInsights:"deduplication-only"` framing, the explicit trigger seam in the commit route). Two user sign-offs obtained this session: (1) the `insight_runs` table (post-Phase-A schema addition), (2) retiring the 8 seeded demo insights now (removed from seed.ts + dev DB reseeded).
**Choice:**
- **`insight_runs` table** (migration 0004): id, patient_id (FK cascade), started_at, trigger jsonb, status running/succeeded/failed, generated_count, error_code, model_version. Exists because empty runs — the most common outcome by §5.6 design — write no insight rows, so `MAX(insights.generated_at)` cannot debounce them. Doubles as concurrency guard (a live `running` row blocks; >5min-old `running` is treated as crashed and doesn't block forever) and audit trail for a fire-and-forget agent.
- **Entity ID directory in vault context** (`includeEntityIdDirectory`, default false): the agent's structured output (`cited_sources`/`linked_entities`) needs real UUIDs but the canonical serialization is slug-only. New opt-in section inverts the existing bijective `slugIndex` (`- § med:glimepiride → type: med, id: <uuid>`), excluding `insight:`/`patient:`/`lifestyle:`/`lab-result:` (insights are never citable evidence; labs cite the parent lab-report). Additive — default output verified byte-free of the section, so synthesis/extraction prompts and their cache keys are untouched. Rejected: threading `(id:…)` through ~13 serializers (regression surface); slug-emission + server resolution (changes the locked wire shape).
- **Agent** (`lib/agents/insight-generator.ts`, `claude-opus-4-7`, mirrors extraction.ts): prompt composed from §5.6+§10.3+§7.1 — 5 locked types, restraint charter (`{"insights":[]}` is the default-correct answer), conservative severity, trigger-relevance, anti-echo-chamber (priors dedup-only), two-layer citations (body `§ slug` pills / structured UUIDs from the directory). Parse uses per-insight salvage: loose envelope, then per-element safeParse — one malformed insight can't discard a run that also found a real one.
- **Orchestrator** (`lib/insights/generate.ts`): debounce (30s) → run row → agent → normalize type vocabulary (`medication`→`med` etc.) → validate every ref via new `entityRefsInScope` (covers the 10 link types + `vital` + `journal`) — invalid refs dropped, zero-evidence insights dropped whole, ≤3 per run → insert with server-stamped `triggeredBy` + `modelVersion` → finish run row. Never throws (fire-and-forget contract). `lib/insights/schedule.ts` wraps it in `after()` (the chat-route auto-titling precedent) so routes never block on Opus.
- **Triggers (server-side, 13 sites):** POST creates for conditions/medications/allergies/visits/lab-reports/vital-readings/symptom-episodes/journal; change POSTs for medication/condition/allergy/lifestyle; medication discontinue; extraction-commit finalize (first committed non-doctor entity, commit vocabulary mapped to ref types). Excluded: doctors, family-history, symptom-types, report metadata. Plus a thin `POST /api/insights/generate` (202; trigger validated in patient scope; patientId auth-derived).
**Deviations flagged (from spec):** (1) agent output drops `triggered_by` — server stamps it; a model echo is a fabrication vector (edit to the "locked" `insightSchema`). (2) `external_refs` suppressed in v1 — no web-search tool; prompt forbids URLs and the orchestrator strips any emitted refs (consistent with the 2026-07-19 external-citations deferral); §5.6 "same web-search rules as synthesis" deferred with it. (3) Leading-edge 30s debounce per the §9.3 sketch vs §5.6's "after a batch settles" — trailing edge needs delayed execution v1 doesn't have; bursts already settle upstream (a multi-card commit is ONE finalize). (4) Dedup context is the full non-dismissed insight history, not §5.6's 90-day window — existing `buildVaultContext` behavior, fine at the intended volume; revisit with a window if it grows. (5) Triggers fire server-side via `after()`, not the §9.3 frontend-fetch sketch — §9.6 auth precedent (patientId never client-supplied). (6) Server caps 3 insights/run (not in spec; restraint belt-and-suspenders). (7) `medications/[id]/discontinue` added as a trigger beyond the planned route list — it appends a change-log row semantically; stopping a med is exactly §5.6-significant.
**Known accepted risks:** `after()`+Opus duration needs `maxDuration` attention at Vercel deploy time (dev unaffected); debounce check-then-insert race on simultaneous POSTs → rare double run (single-user acceptable); every clinical create costs a full-vault Opus call even for `[]` (debounce is the guard); journal/vital cited sources render inert (no detail hrefs — existing section behavior).
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean. Migration SQL = enum + table + FK + index only; applied to arogya-dev. Reseed: 0 insights, seed-phase-e rows gone. `insight-generator:check` (new, self-cleaning): live run generated 1 well-formed insight ([watch/gap] "Metformin at full dose with eGFR now 50" — conservative severity, valid refs, server-stamped trigger, § citations in body), debounce skip asserted, run-row bookkeeping asserted. Regressions: `vault-context:check` (all assertions; default output contains no ID directory), `synthesis:check` (cache hit intact — 12,659 tokens read), `extraction:check`, `citation-parser:check`, `chat-queries:check` all pass.

**Date:** 2026-07-21
**Decision:** **Vitals get their read surface as part of E0a (dashboard), shaped as trend views — NOT as a standalone §6.6 timeline page. Plus: rail reorder, Chat above Insights.**
**Context:** First manual E5 testing surfaced that insight cited-sources render vital readings as inert text while every other type is clickable, and that vitals have no browse surface anywhere (capture form + symptom-episode "Captured at this episode" only). Investigated whether the missing vitals page was an oversight: it isn't. The design deliberately excludes vitals from the page model and the nine-item rail — §4:412 frames them as a different species ("no source document, higher frequency"), §3:145 is explicit that "the trend is the value" (a lone reading has no meaning to navigate to), and §3's rail is capped at nine items for clutter reasons with vitals being the highest-volume entity. Vitals' intended home was always trend visualization on summary surfaces: the dashboard's "key markers card with sparklines + trend arrows" (§6.1:1153) and the profile's "`history →` link inline next to time-series values" (§6.10:1700) — the profile's weight-trend section is already stubbed out in code waiting for exactly this (patient-medical-profile-section.tsx:34).
**Why it surfaced now:** (a) vitals' intended home, the dashboard, is the one prerequisite surface still unbuilt (E0a); (b) E5 created a need the design never anticipated — insights cite INDIVIDUAL readings by id, and a citation needs a landing spot even though browsing doesn't.
**Choice:**
1. Fold the vitals surface into **E0a's scope**: the §6.1 key-markers card (sparklines + trend arrows) as the primary home, and its `history →` affordance targets a **vitals history view grouped by reading type** (per-type table + trend — BP, weight, glucose…), NOT a month-grouped §6.6 card timeline (30 near-identical "BP 152/95" cards is the noisiest page in the app and cuts against "the trend is the value").
2. After that view exists, add a `vital` case to `resolveTypeBatch` (db/queries/entity-links.ts) targeting it (filtered by reading type / anchored to the reading) — insight cited-sources and symptom-linked vitals become clickable with zero changes on the insight side. Until then the inert-snippet rendering stands (documented posture, entity-links.ts:29).
3. **Rail reorder (shipped now):** Chat above Insights in wiki-rail.tsx top-level surfaces — user preference, and consistent with §6.1's hierarchy (chat is the primary affordance; insights sit below it). tsc/eslint clean.
**Alternatives considered:** standalone §6.6 vitals timeline page + tenth rail item, shippable immediately (my first suggestion) — rejected after reading the doc's actual intent: wrong index shape for time-series, re-opens the nine-item rail cap, and would ship days before E0a builds the correct home anyway. Also considered linking cited vitals to the capture form or symptom episode — rejected (misleading destinations).
**Reasoning:** the citation-discipline promise ("every § pill resolves") eventually requires a vitals destination, but the right destination is the one the design already specified. E0a is next in the build order regardless, so the gap closes at the correct layer with no throwaway surface.

**Date:** 2026-07-21
**Decision:** **Rail unread badge for insights (`N new`, accent tint) + the §4:583 `new`→`seen` transition implemented on FEED load (deviation: §4 words it "on dashboard load" — E0a doesn't exist yet; the dashboard will reuse the same helper).**
**Context:** User request after first live E5 use: the rail gives no signal that the generator produced something. §4:583's status lifecycle exists exactly for this, but its automatic first transition was never built anywhere — without it a badge would linger until the user manually acted on every insight.
**Choice:** `WikiCounts.insightsNew` (status='new' count) → accent-tinted badge on the rail's Insights item (pill convention: `bg-accent`/`accent-foreground`, matching vault citation pills). New `insightQueries.markAllSeen(patientId)`; the feed page fires it via `after()` when any `new` insights rendered — the current render still shows the NEW grouping and badge, both clear on next navigation (standard unread semantics), and a failed write silently retries on the next visit. E0a's dashboard calls the same helper when built.
**Alternatives considered:** badge-only without the transition — rejected (permanent badge = alarm fatigue, the exact failure §5.6's restraint calibration guards against); marking seen synchronously in the RSC render — rejected (write-during-render, and it would flip the NEW group before the user ever saw it grouped as new).
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean.

**Date:** 2026-07-22
**Decision:** **Drawer quick-log brought to parity with the full-screen chat: nudge + declined handling, and post-commit `returnTo` targets the CHAT SESSION (full-screen), not the page behind the drawer.**
**Context:** User bug report with transcript: a knee-pain log got the guided-scribe enrichment question, but the flow navigated to the §6.11 confirmation before it could be seen/answered — then the transcript showed the orphaned question followed by the log-ack. Root cause: the DRAWER's `runLog` predates guided-scribe step 2 — it cast the quick-log response as `{extractionSessionId}` and navigated unconditionally, discarding the `nudge`/`question`/`declined` fields the server returned (the server had already persisted the question as an assistant turn, hence the transcript). Latent sibling bug: a `declined` response (no extractionSessionId) would have pushed to `/extract/undefined`. progress.md's "Drawer NOT wired" note was stale — it was router-wired without the E-later nudge branches.
**Choice:**
1. Drawer `runLog` now mirrors `chat-conversation.tsx`: `declined` → advisory inline, stay; `nudge` → question + "Just log it →"/"Never mind" chip, typed input becomes the ANSWER (`reuseSessionId` re-extract), navigation waits.
2. **Post-commit return-to-chat (user request):** drawer `navToConfirm` sets `returnTo=/patient/[id]/chat/[sid]` — the drawer's local conversation can't survive navigation, and the session (with the synthesis log-ack) is where continuation naturally lives. Falls back to the current page only if session creation failed. Full-screen path already returned to its session; both surfaces now converge on §6.2:1203 "the conversation continues."
3. `newSession=1` cleanup param threaded through the drawer too (was full-screen-only) — a session created solely for a fully-discarded log gets deleted, not orphaned.
**Alternatives considered:** extracting a shared `useQuickLog` hook for both surfaces — the right long-term shape (this is the second copy of the logic) but a larger refactor than a bug fix warrants mid-Phase-E; queued as a follow-up. Reopening the drawer post-commit instead of going full-screen — rejected: drawer state doesn't survive navigation, so it would reopen empty.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean (exhaustive-deps warning fixed properly, not suppressed).

**Date:** 2026-07-22
**Decision:** **Shared `useQuickLog` hook (`components/chat/use-quick-log.ts`) — the follow-up queued in the previous entry, done. The full-screen pane and the Ask-AI drawer now consume ONE implementation of the §5.5→§6.11 client flow.**
**Context:** The drawer bug fixed earlier today was caused by exactly this duplication: two hand-rolled copies of classify/log/nudge/declined/retry logic that drifted. User called it: "it doesn't make sense to copy over functionality between main chat and drawer chat."
**Choice:** Hook owns classify dispatch, quick-log POST + its three outcomes, nudge answer/skip lifecycle, disambiguator pending state, failure retry, and confirmation navigation (returnTo always the persisted session). Surfaces own what genuinely differs: their `useChat` instance (injected `setMessages`), `ensureSession` (different creation side-effects), `runQuestion` (different sendMessage bodies), `beforeNavigate` (drawer closes itself), `fallbackReturnTo` (chat index vs page-under-drawer), input/busy state, all rendering. Net −488 duplicated lines; the drawer also gained the quick-log failure/retry UI it never had (hook parity for free).
**Alternatives considered:** a shared component instead of a hook — rejected, the two surfaces' layouts are genuinely different; only the flow logic is common.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean; no server-side changes (quick-log lib/route untouched).

**Date:** 2026-07-22
**Decision:** **E0a shipped — dashboard (§6.1 locked layout) + vitals home (grouped history view + entity-links `vital` case) + rail Dashboard item + root redirect. Browser-verified (15✅) against the real manually-entered vault.**
**Context:** The last prerequisite surface (blocks E4 onboarding). Scope included the 2026-07-21 vitals-home decision: key-markers card as vitals' primary surface, `history →` to a per-type view, then clickable vital citations.
**Choice:**
- **Dashboard** `app/patient/[id]/dashboard/page.tsx` — header card (name/age/active-conditions/doctors/last-activity via `atAGlance`) · key-markers stat tiles (sparklines, de-emphasis ink with accent current-period; **neutral trend arrows** — coloring direction would be a clinical judgment, the per-reading `flag` owns that) · current-meds w/ prescribing-doctor pills · tinted top-insight (`view all N →`; untinted empty per §6.1's polish note) · recent-timeline (6-entity merge in `db/queries/dashboard.ts`) · chat bar at bottom. §4:583 `new`→`seen` now fires here for the displayed top insight (feed keeps its own displayed-only pass).
- **Vitals history** `app/patient/[id]/vitals/page.tsx` — per-type sections (trend chart + table, `#r-<id>` row anchors w/ `target:` highlight), NOT a §6.6 timeline. Shared trend math in `lib/vitals.ts` `groupReadingsByType` (canonical labels/order moved there so the DB layer can compose vital labels — `vital-options` re-exports). Entity-links `vital` → `/vitals#r-<id>`: insight cited-sources + triggered-by now clickable (insight detail's inert-vital fallback deleted); episode "Captured" rows link; profile weight `history →` (closes that §6.10:1678 deviation). Episode-card `●` pills stay inert (card is the anchor — nested-`<a>`).
- **Wiring:** rail Dashboard item on top; `/` redirects to the dashboard; `recentActivity` cuts off future-dated events at patient-timezone today (a scheduled visit was leading the card and rendering "last activity in 2 months" — caught in browser verification).
**Deviations flagged:** meds+insight two-up on lg (§6.1 lists cards sequentially — layout judgment); empty-state ships per-card dashed pattern only (activation banner + `setup N of 4` counter + adaptive chips/placeholder → Phase F per §10.2 item 5); markSeen scope = displayed insight only (§4 wording is "on dashboard load"); "by doctor" meds-header polish item still open.
**Alternatives considered:** marking all insights seen on dashboard load — rejected (feed precedent: never flip what wasn't displayed); §6.6 timeline page for vitals — already rejected 2026-07-21.
**Reasoning:** §1.3 north-star stage: trend + insight + timeline + grounded chat on one orientation surface, and the citation promise ("every § pill resolves") finally holds for vitals.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean; Playwright walkthrough 15✅/3🔍 (root redirect, rail active-state/order, all six cards vs live API data, history→ nav, BP dual-series chart + range, row-anchor highlight, weight history→ href); console clean; probe sessions cleaned up. `/check` pass same session: 5 concerns found+fixed (shared `groupReadingsByType`, DashboardCard reuse in top-insight empty, shared `previewContext`, top-of-file import, **AskAiButton + `VITALS_HISTORY_SURFACE` added to the vitals page** per §6.4:1354).

**Date:** 2026-07-22
**Decision:** **Dashboard chat bar opens the Ask-AI DRAWER (`openWithMessage`), not a full-screen handoff — and the drawer gains "Open full screen ↗". Supersedes the same-day `?draft=` full-screen handoff (built, verified, then replaced; plumbing removed).**
**Context:** First build handed dashboard questions to `/chat?draft=…` (auto-send on mount). User: the §6.1 point is asking with the just-scanned cards still in view — the beside-the-page drawer pattern every entity page already has. Also asked for a way to continue in full-screen beyond the rail's Chat item.
**Choice:**
1. `useChatDrawer().openWithMessage(text, {isQuestion?})` — opens + dispatches in one call. Typed input travels the §5.5 classify path (a typed BP reading quick-logs correctly); chips skip the router (canned-chips precedent). Latest-ref dispatch (effect-assigned per the no-ref-writes-in-render lint) keeps the context value stable; busy → text parks in the drawer input rather than being dropped. Bar publishes new `DASHBOARD_SURFACE`.
2. **"Open full screen ↗"** in the drawer header once a session exists: navigates to `/chat/[sessionId]` (pure navigation — drawer sessions already persist). Disabled mid-stream (navigation would abort the reply before its post-flush persist); on pop-out the drawer relinquishes the conversation (clears local state) so a later drawer open can't silently append to a session whose newer turns it can't see. Works from every page's drawer, not just the dashboard.
3. All `?draft=` plumbing removed (chat page/FullScreenChat/ChatConversation net-zero diff).
**Alternatives considered:** embedded conversation on the dashboard (rejected — page already dense; two conversation implementations); chips→full-screen + typed→drawer split (rejected — unpredictable split behavior; §6.3's scan-routes-to-full-screen precedent noted, escape hatch is the pop-out); keeping `?draft=` alongside (rejected — dead path).
**Reasoning:** One mental model — chat opens beside what you're looking at; sessions persist so nothing is trapped in the drawer.
**Known accepted risks:** pop-out clicked at stream-end can race the `after()` persist by sub-seconds (reload self-heals); drawer header additions match that file's pre-existing `stone-*` literals (token debt lives there, flagged not half-fixed).
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean; Playwright 8✅ (bar→drawer dispatch, dashboard visible beside drawer, pop-out disabled-then-live, lands on transcript, drawer starts fresh after pop-out, chip dispatch); console clean; probe sessions incl. one empty orphan cleaned.

**Date:** 2026-07-22
**Decision:** **E4 shipped — onboarding interview (§5.8 agent + §6.3 surface): `lib/agents/onboarding.ts` (Sonnet, streaming dual output), `onboarding_sessions` infra table (user sign-off), live-transparency entity writes reusing the E3 commit engine, 50/50 surface at `/onboarding`, dashboard activation-banner shell, `?run=scan` health-scan handoff.**
**Context:** The last unbuilt agent. §5.8 and §6.3/§10.3 disagree on the phase list (phase 6 recent-events vs family_history; phase 8 primary-concern vs loose_ends) — **user approved following §6.3/§10.3** (the later, prescriptive sections; family_history otherwise never gets an intake path; the primary-concern journal entry survives inside loose_ends).
**Choice:**
- **Persistence (schema sign-off, AskUserQuestion):** new `onboarding_sessions` infra table — singleton per patient (unique index), `status` (`active`/`completed`), `current_phase` (9-value enum incl. `complete`), transcript as jsonb messages. Transcript deliberately NOT in `chat_sessions`: the interview is an intake flow, not a findable conversation, and must never appear in the CHATS list. Migration 0005, additive only.
- **Dual output on the wire:** the agent streams prose interleaved with ```` ```entity ````/```` ```phase ```` fenced JSON. Server transform (`lib/onboarding/stream.ts` FenceParser → `app/api/onboarding` ndjson events): text forwards immediately, each completed entity fence is Zod-validated → written → `{t:"entity"}` event; fences never reach the user; a malformed/unterminated block drops with a code-only log, never fails the turn. Client is a plain fetch/reader loop — no useChat (no router/quick-log plumbing to inherit).
- **Live-transparency writes (§5.8:1023 — the sanctioned no-gate path):** `lib/onboarding/commit.ts` routes the 8 extraction types through the E3 `commitCard` engine (change-log discipline + never-fabricate coercion for free) with `CommitContext.reportId` widened to `string | null` (no source document → `source_report_id` null). Onboarding-only mappers: patient identity (UPDATE of the seeded row — phase 1 live header), family_history (unrecognized relation → `other` with the user's words in `relation_specific`), lifestyle (upsert; `field_locked` → refuse, never overwrite trend history), journal_entry (the loose-ends concern).
- **Surface:** `app/onboarding` — OUTSIDE the patient layout (focused takeover, no rail, per the §6.3 sketch). Chat left (6.2 visuals: ✦ AiMessage, accent user bubbles, `•••`), `STEP N OF 8` strip + `Skip to dashboard →`, persistent escape chips; panel right (live counts, ~1s accent flash on fresh cards via snapshot-id diffing, `● live · building`, `click any field to edit`). Panel refetches GET `/api/onboarding` per entity event + on window focus.
- **Completion:** agent emits `{"phase":"complete"}` → status flips; UI renders deterministic chips (`Run the full health scan →` / `Go to dashboard →`) under the agent's conversational offer — no yes-parsing. Scan chip → `/patient/[id]/chat?run=scan`: server resolves the param to the fixed prompt (never client free text), `ChatConversation` autoruns it once on an empty draft via the existing `runQuestion`. One debounced `scheduleInsightGeneration` fires at completion with the last committed clinical entity (per-emission firing would only re-arm the 30s debounce all interview).
- **Banner shell (§6.3:1249):** `ActivationBanner` on the dashboard while no completed session — the interview's ONLY launcher ("Walk me through it →"). `setup · N of 4` counter + adaptive logic stay Phase F.
**Deviations flagged:**
- **`Upload instead` keeps the E3 confirmation gate** (new tab → §6.11 screen; panel focus-refetch shows committed cards). §6.3:1258 wants results materializing gate-free on the panel; kept conservative side of the extraction-never-auto-writes tripwire. Revisit if the extra tab hurts.
- **Inline edit scope:** primary text field per card (name/substance/condition_name; patient name) via `InlinePatchField` (useState+onBlur → existing PATCH routes). Enum/date/secondary fields edit on the entity's own page. §6.3's "every field" is deferred polish.
- **§6.3's "terra/clay" accent** rendered with the locked periwinkle semantic tokens (accent lock 2026-05-31 postdates the sketch).
- Prompt hardening from live smoke testing: (1) matching dictionary lacks the patient row → identity line added to phase context (agent was re-asking phase 1 on a populated vault; now confirms/fills gaps and skips covered phases — observed jumping straight to family_history); (2) required-minimums section (med needs name+dose+frequency, doctor name+specialty, …) — agent must ask before emitting and NEVER claim a capture that was refused (observed "noted Dr. Faketest" while the specialty-less doctor emission was refused).
**Alternatives considered:** `chat_sessions.kind` column (rejected — leaks the interview into every history query); structured-output whole-turn JSON (rejected — kills streamed feel; partial-JSON parsing fragile); `useChat` + data parts (rejected — inherits plumbing the interview doesn't want); per-emission insight triggers (rejected — debounce re-arming).
**Reasoning:** The activation moment IS the product's hardest problem (§5.8); everything reuses the vault's existing write discipline so the interview can't drift from the record's rules.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean; migration applied + table inspected; live smoke: GET snapshot against the real vault; opening turn streamed (phase fence → event, greeting streamed, transcript persisted, synthetic opening turn correctly not persisted); second turn committed a test medication live (`ok:true` + id + label) and correctly refused a specialty-less doctor; banner renders on dashboard; `?run=scan` route 200; test rows (med + session) deleted after.

**Date:** 2026-07-23
**Decision:** **E4 browser-verified (Playwright, 5 runs vs the real vault) — 12✅/2🔍 net; three fixes landed during verification: same-turn-capture worked example in the prompt, no-inferred-relationship rule, `nativeButton={false}` on the completion chip links.**
**Context:** /verify-ui onboarding. Live Sonnet turns make steps probabilistic; the walkthrough was iterated until failures were explained, not just green.
**What verification caught (all fixed):**
1. **Agent refuses test-data framing AND implausible drug names** ("ZZZverifymed… not a medicine name I can place → Upload instead"). Kept — that's the never-fabricate posture working; the harness now uses a real med (Cilnidipine) tracked by ID for cleanup. House-rule note for future runs: ZZZ-prefix naming does not survive an agent with integrity — track by ID.
2. **Capture lagged a turn behind** — the model held emissions while asking optional follow-ups (purpose/prescriber, add-vs-replace), violating §5.8's capture-first pattern. Instruction alone didn't fix it; a **worked example** in the prompt did (verified: same-turn emission + follow-up riding along).
3. **"your father" without a stated relationship** — synthesis's naming addendum had no onboarding equivalent. Prompt line added; greeting now asks the relationship instead of assuming.
4. **Base UI console error on completion chips** — `Button render={<Link>}` needs `nativeButton={false}`; note the fix flips the anchor's exposed role to `button` (harness selectors updated accordingly).
**Harness learnings (skill-relevant):** Playwright's `response.finished()` can stall on the ndjson stream even after the server closes (curl confirms prompt close) — bound it or wait on UI state (chips enabled) instead; buffered `| tail` hides all output until exit — log steps to a side file.
**Verified green:** fresh-load auto-opening turn (streams, phase strip, §6.3 chrome) · resume on reload with no duplicate turn · live med capture → panel card + count, same turn · inline rename persists · escape chips · Skip-to-dashboard round trip with banner while active · banner gone on completion · completion chips (8 OF 8 · DONE) · `?run=scan` handoff with synthesis streaming · strict-schema 400 probes · cleanup verified (patient row byte-identical to pre-test snapshot, med/chat/session counts restored, test interview deleted so the activation banner is back).
**Screenshots:** `~/Desktop/arogya-verify-shots/onb-*.png` (opening, live card, completion, scan).

**Date:** 2026-07-29
**Decision:** **/check pass on E4 — one blocker found + fixed (completion insight trigger never fired), 4 hardening fixes, §5.8:1043 implemented, 2 deviations logged as deferred.**
**Blocker (two stacked causes, both fixed + live-verified):**
1. `after()` called from the detached stream pump is silently dropped — no request scope. Evidence: no `insight_runs` row from the 07-23 verified completion. Fix: register `after()` in the handler body with a deferred promise the pump resolves at stream end.
2. The trigger ref was per-TURN — a real interview's final turn ("we're done") commits nothing, so even a working `after()` would no-op. Fix: `onboardingSessionQueries.latestClinicalRef` (most recently created among med/condition/vital/journal) as the completion fallback; an empty vault correctly skips (nothing to analyze).
Verified live 2026-07-29: completion turn → `insight_runs` row `succeeded · generated_count 0 · trigger journal` — the generator ran and correctly decided nothing new was worth surfacing (§5.6 empty-output default doing its job).
**Hardening (all from the /check principal review):** `appendTurn` → single-statement jsonb concat (concurrent turns append, not clobber) · panel refresh debounced 300ms trailing (bulk captures were a refetch-per-entity query burst) · `InlinePatchField` keyed on value (agent-side corrections remount instead of losing to a stale local draft) · family-history/journal emissions honor `intent:"update"` + `matched_entity_id` (corrections no longer duplicate rows; shared `familyHistoryValues` coercer) · wire event type unified — client imports `OnboardingWireEvent` from `lib/onboarding/stream.ts`.
**§5.8:1043 implemented:** route passes days-since-last-turn; ≥7 days adds a phase-context line so the agent opens with resume-or-dashboard.
**Deferred (logged, not built):** §6.3:1262 `follow-up` tag on entity cards → Phase F polish; §10.1 mobile "best on desktop" guidance → app-wide gap, not E4-specific, fix later (user call 2026-07-29).
**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean; live completion turn end-to-end; test session deleted after (banner restored, vault untouched).

**Date:** 2026-07-29
**Decision:** **Onboarding panel scroll-into-view — the topmost freshly-captured card smooth-scrolls into view before its highlight plays (`block:"nearest"`, so a visible card never causes a jump); flash window 1.2s→1.8s to absorb the scroll.**
**Context:** User's manual pass: captures were landing below the fold on the populated vault — the §6.3:1235 flash played off-screen and live-transparency read as "nothing happened."

**Date:** 2026-07-30
**Decision:** **Onboarding update-target ids for family history + journal — `buildOnboardingUpdateTargets` appended to the agent's dictionary; prompt now names the follow-up-answer-is-an-update rule explicitly.**
**Context:** User's real interview: "father had heart disease" → create; answered the outcome follow-up ("death") → agent emitted a SECOND create → duplicate rows. Root cause: the shared matching dictionary (§5.4) deliberately omits family history/journal (not extraction match targets), so the agent had no id to aim an update at — the commit-side update path (2026-07-29) was necessary but unreachable. Duplicate deleted (kept the outcome-bearing row).
**Choice:** onboarding-local supplement (family-history entries + 5 recent journal entries, with UUIDs), NOT an extension of `buildMatchingDictionary` — extraction doesn't handle those types and its dictionary shouldn't grow non-targets. Prompt: answers to the agent's own follow-ups about an already-emitted entry are updates carrying only the answered field.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint; runtime print blocked by `server-only` (correct fencing); live proof deferred to the user's next in-interview correction — dev server hot-reloads, so their active session picks this up on its next turn.

**Date:** 2026-07-30
**Decision:** **E4 stress pass (user-requested after the colon-cancer duplicate): 17/18 API scenarios + 9/9 UI green; one new failure mode found + fixed (claimed-but-unwritten update); updated cards now flash/scroll via event-id highlights.**
**Suite (one continuous live interview, ndjson-level assertions):** bulk capture (2 meds + condition in one paragraph) · existing-doctor dictionary match (no dup) · med follow-up answers → update events targeting existing ids · family-history follow-up (the regression) → no dup · re-mention recap → no dup · vague "small white tablet" → no fabrication · named med w/o dose → no emission · deletion request → refused, row untouched · client abort mid-stream → clean recovery next turn · concurrent turns → both transcripts survive (atomic appendTurn earning its keep) · zero writes outside expected types. UI: correction edits the card IN PLACE (10mg→20mg), single card throughout, updated card flashes, zero page errors.
**New failure mode (S5):** agent said "Noted — diagnosed in her fifties, passed away at 82" while emitting NOTHING — silent data loss with a confident acknowledgement. Same compliance gap as capture-first, update flavor. Fix: second worked example (follow-up answer → update emission with matched id), "every noted/captured MUST carry the matching emission in the same turn", fuzzy values stay fuzzy (approximate timing → notes, numeric fields only for stated numbers). Retest: update event on the same id; row filled (onset 70 · deceased · 15-years detail in notes).
**UI change:** highlights now key on entity-event ids, not just new-id diffs — UPDATED cards flash + scroll into view (they were invisible before, same complaint class as 2026-07-30 scroll fix).
**Test hygiene:** full pre-run backups (session transcript via row_to_json/jsonb_populate_record round-trip — pg_dump blocked by 14-vs-17 version mismatch; patient + lifestyle rows), created_at-window sweeps, tracked-id deletes. Post-run: real transcript restored (7 messages, family_history phase), patient + lifestyle byte-identical, all counts at baseline.
**Notable:** the agent spontaneously flagged Rosuvastatin vs the vault's existing Atorvastatin (two statins) during a test turn — cross-record reasoning showing up unprompted, exactly the §1.3 muscle.

**Date:** 2026-08-03
**Decision:** **Onboarding context completeness closed (rehearsal findings): lifestyle summary + patient extras in the agent's context; shared matching dictionary enriched with med purpose/prescriber (resolved to names), condition severity, allergy reaction/severity.**
**Context:** From-scratch rehearsal on the populated vault: agent re-asked the fully-filled lifestyle phase (invisible to it — not an extraction type, not in the supplement). Audit then found the same class three more times: patient extras (preferred name/blood type/height/weight) absent from the identity line; med purpose/prescriber stored as unresolved uuids; condition severity + allergy reaction absent.
**Choice:** lifestyle values + patient extras go in onboarding's own context; the med/condition/allergy detail goes in the SHARED dictionary — extraction benefits identically (matching "the BP tablet Dr. Menon gave him" needs the same resolution), modest token cost. Fourth instance of the context-blindness class; every entity type the interview touches is now represented with the fields it would otherwise re-ask.
**Verified:** tsc + eslint; live behavior lands on the user's next interview turn (hot reload).

**Context:** User answered lifestyle questions and saw no highlight — writes were landing correctly but the two singleton areas (lifestyle, patient identity) had no flash machinery (no data-entity-id, ids absent from the highlight bookkeeping), and stress_context wasn't in the snapshot's field list at all. Verified fixed by driving the user's real completed session with two truthful turns (stress-context restore + identity extras): both flashed, both wrote.
**Also:** §5.8 post-completion additions work naturally (agent accepts, re-offers the scan). Family-history identity rule (2026-08-04, uncommitted until now): (relative, condition) pair — new condition for a known relative is a CREATE; fixed the note-stuffing failure ("Also had Type 2 Diabetes" appended to the colon-cancer row's notes).
**Reconciliation:** calcium+D3 and elder-brother rows recreated (tmp purge ate the backup file — lesson: rehearsal backups belong in a persistent dir, not /tmp; all values were recoverable from session output); med purposes relinked (Atorvastatin→Dyslipidemia, Tamsulosin→BPH, Shallaki→Osteoarthritis). Open user calls: diet restrictions + tobacco (live interview answers contradict the pre-rehearsal record; live answers currently stand); Aspirin purpose/prescribers unset (never captured).
